# Phase 6 Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Chat controls (delete, pin, undo, branch, busy policy, reasoning/fast), the in-turn prompts (password manager, connection card), and the restyle of the unmocked features to the Phase 2 design.

**Architecture:** New wire calls go on `ChatBackend`/`DashboardChat`; new server requests and events become sealed `ChatEvent`s that `ChatState.apply` folds in; `ChatController` owns the new behaviour (undo, branch, queue tray, prompts) and the screens stay thin. Device-local data (pins, branch links, recent searches, image hosts) lives on the existing Preferences table behind small Riverpod notifiers. New UI is built from existing tokens and primitives; the design file is the geometry reference.

**Tech Stack:** Flutter (Dart 3), Riverpod 3 (hand-written providers), Drift (Preferences table), `package:markdown`, `url_launcher`, `flutter_test`.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-16-phase6-design.md`. **Design:** `design/Iris Phase 2.dc.html` (frames A1–A8, B1–B10, Motion). Open it in a browser, or read it as text: every frame has a "Notes" block with sizes, radii, springs and copy.

## Global Constraints

- Invoke the `flutter` skill before the first task and follow its verification loop. Windows build traps apply.
- After **every** task: `flutter analyze` (0 issues) and `flutter test` (all green; `live` and `live_write` tests skip themselves). Then commit with a conventional message (`feat(phase6): …`). **Commits are under the user's name only: no `Co-Authored-By` trailer, no Claude-Session line.** Don't push.
- Never weaken a test to make it pass. If a selector no longer fits (for example `find.text('Steer')` becomes `find.bySemanticsLabel('Steer')`), change the selector so it still checks the same behaviour. Contrast tests are never loosened; adjust the colour.
- Match the surrounding code: library doc comments, comments that explain *why*, `context.palette` / `context.type` / `context.iris`, `IrisSpace` / `IrisRadius` / `IrisSize` tokens, `IrisPressable` for taps, `Result`/`Ok`/`Err` for fallible calls.
- Every colour is a palette role or an `AgentState` colour (`palette.colorFor(AgentState.waiting)` is the needs-you colour). No design hex value appears in a widget. Tints are `colour.withValues(alpha: …)`.
- Copy is plain, short British English. No "coming soon" and no placeholders.
- Every tappable reserves a 44×44 hit area. Every new surface renders without overflow at 320 pt wide and 200% text.
- Reduced motion: every new animation has a cross-fade or an instant alternative. Tests run with `disableAnimations: true`; under it no new ticker may run, or `pumpAndSettle` hangs.
- **Typed secrets never persist**: a password, one-time code, login or credential goes straight into the reply frame. It is never put in `ChatState`, a transcript item, a notice, a log line or a preference.
- Wire fields are read through `core/json.dart` and never trusted: unknown values are kept or ignored, never thrown on.
- Any test that finds a widget with `find.bySemanticsLabel` must call `final semantics = tester.ensureSemantics();` first and `semantics.dispose();` last, as the existing tests do.
- Limits that already exist and must not change: at most 5 attachments per message, 20 MB each.
- Preference keys (spec): `pins` (scope `<server>/<profile>`, JSON list of stored ids, newest first), `branch.<childId>` (scope `<server>/<profile>`, JSON `{parent, parentTitle, count}`), `search.recent` (scope server id, JSON list, at most 6, newest first), `images.hosts` (scope server id, JSON list).
- Wire (verified in Hermes `main`, 2026-10-16): `session.undo {session_id}` → `{removed}`; `session.branch_stored {parent_session_id, profile?}`; `session.create {parent_session_id, messages:[{role,content}], title, profile?}`; `session.compress {session_id}`; `session.active_list {}` → `{sessions:[{id, session_key, status}]}`; `config.set {key:"busy", value: "status"|"queue"|"steer"|"interrupt"}`; `config.set {key:"reasoning", session_id, value}`; `config.set {key:"fast", session_id, value:"fast"|"normal"}`; `prompt.submit {…, queued:true}` → `{status: streaming|queued|steered|redirected}`; `connection.respond {owner:{type:"session",session_id}, op_id, result:{targets:[{name,status:"approved"|"skipped",detail?,env?}], settled_by?}}`; REST `DELETE /api/sessions/{id}` (profile in the query).

## Review Focus

Failure modes the spec implies that no feature slice names. Each line has a test in the task that owns the code.

1. **A reconnect while the queue tray holds messages.** `_reattach` flips the turn to idle without a completion event; the tray must send exactly one message, never two, and never into a failed or stopped turn. (Task 8.)
2. **A server that lacks a method** (`unknown method` / -32601): undo, branch, compress, busy, reasoning, fast, `session.active_list`. The control must say "not supported on this server" or hide itself, never show a raw RPC error. (Tasks 1, 6, 7, 8, 9.)
3. **Branching from a reply that is not stored yet** (still streaming, or a brand-new chat with no stored id) and from an older reply whose earlier pages are not loaded. The menu hides Branch for the first; the prefix builder loads pages for the second. (Task 7.)
4. **Secrets in state.** After answering an unlock, code, login, sudo or secret prompt, no field of `ChatState`, no transcript item and no notice may contain what was typed. (Task 10.)
5. **A connection payload Iris doesn't recognise** (unknown `kind`/`action`/`state`, a `connection.update` for an op that isn't open, a deadline already past): the card falls back to "Finish this on Hermes Desktop" and never throws or hangs. (Task 11.)
6. **A pinned or branch-linked chat that was deleted elsewhere**: a 404 on the pinned id drops the pin; opening a deleted parent shows the chat screen's own "Could not open this conversation" state. (Tasks 2, 5, 7.)

- `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-16-phase6-probe.md`, `…-phase6-device.md`.

### Task 0: Probe the user's server

**Files:**
- Create: `test/live/phase6_probe_live_test.dart`
- Create: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-16-phase6-probe.md` (the recorded result)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `DashboardSession.call(method, {params})` → `Result<Json>`; `IrisFailure.rpcError?.isMethodNotFound`.
- Produces: a table in the probe doc of which methods the server has. Later tasks use it to decide which fall back to "not supported".

The probe calls each new RPC with an **invalid id** so nothing is changed: "unknown method" means missing, anything else (invalid params, session not found) means present.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the probe**

```dart
/// Which Phase 6 methods the user's Hermes has. Read-only: every call uses an
/// id that does not exist, so a present method answers with an error about the
/// id and a missing one answers "unknown method".
///   flutter test --tags live --run-skipped test/live/phase6_probe_live_test.dart
@Tags(['live'])
library;

import 'dart:io';

import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:iris/core/json.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/net/credentials.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/net/server_config.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/net/server_connection.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/rpc/dashboard_session.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/rpc/rpc_frame.dart';

void main() {
  late DashboardSession session;

  setUp(() async {
    final json = Json.decode(
      File(
        '${Platform.environment['USERPROFILE'] ?? Platform.environment['HOME']}'
        '/.iris-live.json',
      ).readAsStringSync(),
    );
    final connection = ServerConnection.create(
      ServerConfig(
        id: 'live',
        baseUrl: ServerConfig.normaliseBaseUrl(json['base_url'].string()),
        label: 'live',
      ),
      secrets: ServerSecrets(
        username: json['username'].stringOrNull,
        password: json['password'].stringOrNull,
        accessClientId: json['cf_client_id'].stringOrNull,
        accessClientSecret: json['cf_client_secret'].stringOrNull,
      ),
    );
    session = DashboardSession(http: connection);
    addTearDown(() async {
      await session.dispose();
      connection.close();
    });
    await session.start();
    expect(session.state.phase, LinkPhase.live);
  });

  const probes = <String, Map<String, Object?>>{
    'session.undo': {'session_id': 'iris-probe'},
    'session.branch_stored': {'parent_session_id': 'iris-probe'},
    'session.compress': {'session_id': 'iris-probe'},
    'session.active_list': {},
    'connection.respond': {
      'owner': {'type': 'session', 'session_id': 'iris-probe'},
      'op_id': 'iris-probe',
      'result': {},
    },
    'config.set busy': {'key': 'busy', 'value': 'status'},
    'config.set reasoning': {
      'key': 'reasoning',
      'session_id': 'iris-probe',
      'value': 'medium',
    },
    'config.set fast': {
      'key': 'fast',
      'session_id': 'iris-probe',
      'value': 'normal',
    },
  };

  test('which Phase 6 methods exist', () async {
    final lines = <String>[];
    for (final entry in probes.entries) {
      final method = entry.key.split(' ').first;
      final result = await session.call(method, params: entry.value);
      final line = switch (result) {
        _ when result.isOk => '${entry.key}: present (answered)',
        _ when result.failureOrNull?.rpcError?.isMethodNotFound ?? false =>
          '${entry.key}: MISSING',
        _ => '${entry.key}: present '
            '(${result.failureOrNull?.rpcError?.code ?? result.failureOrNull?.kind.name})',
      };
      lines.add(line);
      // ignore: avoid_print
      print(line);
    }
    // The probe's job is the printout; it only fails if nothing answered.
    expect(lines, hasLength(probes.length));
  });
}
```

```dart
/// A connection request, tolerant of every shape Hermes has used.
library;

import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:iris/core/json.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/chat/connection_models.dart';

void main() {
  Json payload() => Json.of({
    'op_id': 'op1',
    'seq': 2,
    'deadline_at': 1900000000.5,
    'timeout_seconds': 120,
    'targets': [
      {
        'name': 'Linear',
        'kind': 'connector',
        'action': 'authorize',
        'state': 'pending',
        'connect_url': 'https://linear.app/oauth',
      },
      {
        'name': 'GitHub',
        'kind': 'mcp',
        'action': 'connect',
        'state': 'pending',
        'required_env': [
          {
            'name': 'GITHUB_TOKEN',
            'required': true,
            'secret': true,
            'default': '',
            'prompt': 'Personal access token with repo scope.',
          },
        ],
      },
      {'name': 'release-notes', 'kind': 'skill', 'action': 'enable', 'state': 'pending'},
      {'name': 'security-scan', 'kind': 'plugin', 'action': 'install', 'state': 'pending'},
      {'name': 'Mystery', 'kind': 'teleporter', 'action': 'beam', 'state': 'glowing'},
    ],
  });

  test('parses targets and picks the control each one needs', () {
    final request = ConnectionRequest.parse(payload(), sessionId: 's1')!;
    expect(request.opId, 'op1');
    expect(request.sessionId, 's1');
    expect(request.targets.map((t) => t.control), [
      TargetControl.signIn,
      TargetControl.credentials,
      TargetControl.approve,
      TargetControl.desktopOnly,
      TargetControl.desktopOnly,
    ]);
    expect(request.targets[1].requiredEnv.single.secret, isTrue);
    expect(request.targets[1].requiredEnv.single.prompt, contains('repo scope'));
  });

  test('unknown kinds, actions and states are kept, not thrown on', () {
    final request = ConnectionRequest.parse(payload(), sessionId: 's1')!;
    final odd = request.targets.last;
    expect(odd.kind, TargetKind.other);
    expect(odd.action, TargetAction.other);
    expect(odd.state, TargetState.other);
    expect(odd.control, TargetControl.desktopOnly);
  });

  test('a resolved target needs no control', () {
    final target = ConnectionTarget.parse(Json.of({
      'name': 'x',
      'kind': 'connector',
      'action': 'authorize',
      'state': 'connected',
      'connect_url': 'https://x.test',
    }))!;
    expect(target.control, TargetControl.none);
  });

  test('no op id means no card', () {
    expect(ConnectionRequest.parse(Json.of({'targets': []}), sessionId: 's'), isNull);
  });

  test('the deadline comes from deadline_at, else from the timeout', () {
    final fixed = DateTime.utc(2030, 1, 1);
    final at = ConnectionRequest.parse(
      Json.of({'op_id': 'a', 'deadline_at': fixed.millisecondsSinceEpoch / 1000}),
      sessionId: 's',
      now: DateTime.utc(2029),
    )!;
    expect(at.deadline.toUtc(), fixed);
    final soon = ConnectionRequest.parse(
      Json.of({'op_id': 'a', 'timeout_seconds': 90}),
      sessionId: 's',
      now: DateTime.utc(2029),
    )!;
    expect(soon.deadline, DateTime.utc(2029).add(const Duration(seconds: 90)));
  });

  test('an update replaces target states by name and can settle', () {
    final request = ConnectionRequest.parse(payload(), sessionId: 's1')!;
    final updated = request.withTargets([
      ConnectionTarget.parse(Json.of({
        'name': 'Linear',
        'kind': 'connector',
        'action': 'authorize',
        'state': 'connected',
      }))!,
    ], settled: false);
    expect(updated.targets.firstWhere((t) => t.name == 'Linear').state, TargetState.connected);
    expect(updated.targets, hasLength(5), reason: 'targets not in the update stay');
    expect(updated.settled, isFalse);
    expect(updated.withTargets(const [], settled: true).settled, isTrue);
  });
}
```

- [ ] **Step A3: Create `lib/data/chat/connection_models.dart`**

```dart
/// The connection card's data: what the agent wants to connect, and the
/// answers Iris sends back.
///
/// Hermes's contract is wide (installs, catalog scans, plugin phases). Iris
/// reads only what the minimal card needs and keeps every enum value it does
/// not know as `other`, so a newer server never breaks the card: such a row
/// simply says to finish on Hermes Desktop.
library;

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';

import '../../core/json.dart';

enum TargetKind {
  connector,
  mcp,
  plugin,
  skill,
  other;

  static TargetKind fromWire(String? value) => switch (value?.trim()) {
    'connector' => connector,
    'mcp' => mcp,
    'plugin' => plugin,
    'skill' => skill,
    _ => other,
  };
}

enum TargetAction {
  authorize,
  connect,
  enable,
  install,
  reconnect,
  other;

  static TargetAction fromWire(String? value) => switch (value?.trim()) {
    'authorize' => authorize,
    'connect' => connect,
    'enable' => enable,
    'install' => install,
    'reconnect' => reconnect,
    _ => other,
  };
}

enum TargetState {
  pending,
  initiated,
  connected,
  skipped,
  failed,
  expired,
  notConnected,
  other;

  static TargetState fromWire(String? value) => switch (value?.trim()) {
    'pending' => pending,
    'initiated' => initiated,
    'connected' => connected,
    'skipped' => skipped,
    'failed' => failed,
    'expired' => expired,
    'not_connected' => notConnected,
    _ => other,
  };

  /// Nothing more to do for this target.
  bool get resolved => switch (this) {
    connected || skipped || failed || expired => true,
    _ => false,
  };
}

/// What a row offers the person, decided from its kind, action and fields.
enum TargetControl {
  /// Resolved: the chip says it all.
  none,

  /// Open the provider's sign-in page in the browser.
  signIn,

  /// Type the credentials the server needs, then Approve.
  credentials,

  /// One tap to approve.
  approve,

  /// Not something a phone can do: Skip only.
  desktopOnly,
}

/// One value an MCP server needs before it can connect.
@immutable
final class EnvField {
  const EnvField({
    required this.name,
    this.required = true,
    this.secret = true,
    this.prompt = '',
    this.fallback = '',
  });

  final String name;
  final bool required;
  final bool secret;

  /// Hermes's hint for the field.
  final String prompt;

  /// The server's default, shown as a placeholder only.
  final String fallback;
}

@immutable
final class ConnectionTarget {
  const ConnectionTarget({
    required this.name,
    required this.kind,
    required this.action,
    required this.state,
    this.detail = '',
    this.instructions = '',
    this.connectUrl,
    this.requiredEnv = const [],
  });

  /// A target, or null without a name (nothing could answer for it).
  static ConnectionTarget? parse(Json json) {
    final name = json['name'].stringOrNull?.trim();
    if (name == null || name.isEmpty) return null;
    final url = json['connect_url'].stringOrNull?.trim();
    return ConnectionTarget(
      name: name,
      kind: TargetKind.fromWire(json['kind'].stringOrNull),
      action: TargetAction.fromWire(json['action'].stringOrNull),
      state: TargetState.fromWire(json['state'].stringOrNull),
      detail: json['detail'].string().trim(),
      instructions: json['instructions'].string().trim(),
      connectUrl: url == null || url.isEmpty ? null : url,
      requiredEnv: [
        for (final field in json['required_env'].list)
          if (field['name'].stringOrNull case final String fieldName
              when fieldName.isNotEmpty)
            EnvField(
              name: fieldName,
              required: field['required'].boolOrNull ?? true,
              secret: field['secret'].boolOrNull ?? true,
              prompt: field['prompt'].string().trim(),
              fallback: field['default'].string(),
            ),
      ],
    );
  }

  final String name;
  final TargetKind kind;
  final TargetAction action;
  final TargetState state;

  /// Why a target failed, or what is happening ("Token rejected").
  final String detail;
  final String instructions;
  final String? connectUrl;
  final List<EnvField> requiredEnv;

  TargetControl get control {
    if (state.resolved) return TargetControl.none;
    final signsIn =
        action == TargetAction.authorize ||
        action == TargetAction.connect ||
        action == TargetAction.reconnect;
    if (connectUrl != null && signsIn) return TargetControl.signIn;
    if (kind == TargetKind.mcp && requiredEnv.isNotEmpty) {
      return TargetControl.credentials;
    }
    if (action == TargetAction.enable &&
        (kind == TargetKind.skill || kind == TargetKind.mcp)) {
      return TargetControl.approve;
    }
    if (kind == TargetKind.mcp &&
        (action == TargetAction.connect || action == TargetAction.install)) {
      return TargetControl.approve;
    }
    return TargetControl.desktopOnly;
  }

  ConnectionTarget withState(TargetState next, {String? detail}) =>
      ConnectionTarget(
        name: name,
        kind: kind,
        action: action,
        state: next,
        detail: detail ?? this.detail,
        instructions: instructions,
        connectUrl: connectUrl,
        requiredEnv: requiredEnv,
      );
}

@immutable
final class ConnectionRequest {
  const ConnectionRequest({
    required this.opId,
    required this.sessionId,
    required this.deadline,
    required this.targets,
    this.settled = false,
  });

  /// From a `connection.request` payload or a resume's `pending_connection`.
  /// Null without an op id.
  static ConnectionRequest? parse(
    Json json, {
    required String sessionId,
    DateTime? now,
  }) {
    final opId = json['op_id'].stringOrNull?.trim();
    if (opId == null || opId.isEmpty) return null;
    final at = json['deadline_at'].doubleOrNull;
    final timeout = json['timeout_seconds'].doubleOrNull ?? 300;
    final start = now ?? DateTime.now();
    return ConnectionRequest(
      opId: opId,
      sessionId: sessionId,
      deadline: at != null && at > 1e9
          ? DateTime.fromMillisecondsSinceEpoch((at * 1000).round(), isUtc: true)
          : start.add(Duration(milliseconds: (timeout * 1000).round())),
      targets: [
        for (final row in json['targets'].list) ?ConnectionTarget.parse(row),
      ],
    );
  }

  final String opId;
  final String sessionId;
  final DateTime deadline;
  final List<ConnectionTarget> targets;
  final bool settled;

  /// This request with [updates] merged in by name (a name not already here
  /// is added), and [settled] set.
  ConnectionRequest withTargets(
    List<ConnectionTarget> updates, {
    required bool settled,
  }) {
    final merged = [...targets];
    for (final update in updates) {
      final at = merged.indexWhere((t) => t.name == update.name);
      if (at < 0) {
        merged.add(update);
      } else {
        merged[at] = update;
      }
    }
    return ConnectionRequest(
      opId: opId,
      sessionId: sessionId,
      deadline: deadline,
      targets: merged,
      settled: settled || this.settled,
    );
  }
}

/// What the person decided for one target.
@immutable
final class TargetAnswer {
  const TargetAnswer.approved(this.name, {this.env = const {}})
    : approved = true;
  const TargetAnswer.skipped(this.name) : approved = false, env = const {};

  final String name;
  final bool approved;

  /// Credential values for an MCP install. Never stored: they go straight
  /// into `connection.respond`.
  final Map<String, String> env;

  Map<String, Object?> toWire() => {
    'name': name,
    'status': approved ? 'approved' : 'skipped',
    if (env.isNotEmpty) 'env': env,
  };
}
```

```dart
/// The agent wants to save a login: a username and password for a site.
/// Answered once and forgotten, like [InputRequest].
@immutable
final class LoginRequest {
  const LoginRequest({
    required this.requestId,
    required this.sessionId,
    required this.origin,
    this.site = '',
  });

  final String requestId;
  final String sessionId;

  /// `https://github.com`.
  final String origin;

  /// Hermes's name for the site, when it has one.
  final String site;

  /// What to call the site: Hermes's name, else the origin's host.
  String get siteName =>
      site.isNotEmpty ? site : (Uri.tryParse(origin)?.host ?? origin);
}

/// What Hermes does with a message sent while a reply is running
/// (`display.busy_input_mode`, profile-wide).
enum BusyMode {
  queue('queue', 'Queue', 'Your message runs after the current reply.'),
  steer('steer', 'Steer', 'It joins the running reply at the next step.'),
  interrupt(
    'interrupt',
    'Interrupt',
    'The reply stops and Hermes answers this instead.',
  );

  const BusyMode(this.wire, this.label, this.blurb);

  final String wire;
  final String label;
  final String blurb;

  /// Hermes's own default is interrupt, so that is what an unknown or
  /// missing value means.
  static BusyMode fromWire(String? value) =>
      switch (value?.trim().toLowerCase()) {
        'queue' => queue,
        'steer' => steer,
        _ => interrupt,
      };
}

/// How hard the model thinks. Hermes also knows `minimal`, `xhigh` and
/// `ultra`; they fold onto the nearest of these five for display.
enum ReasoningEffort {
  none,
  low,
  medium,
  high,
  max;

  String get wire => name;

  String get label => '${name[0].toUpperCase()}${name.substring(1)}';

  static ReasoningEffort? fromWire(String? value) =>
      switch (value?.trim().toLowerCase()) {
        'none' || 'false' || 'disabled' => none,
        'minimal' || 'low' => low,
        'medium' => medium,
        'high' => high,
        'xhigh' || 'max' || 'ultra' => max,
        _ => null,
      };
}

/// What `prompt.submit` did with a message.
enum SubmitOutcome {
  /// A new turn began.
  started,

  /// The session was busy and Hermes held the message for after the turn.
  queued,

  /// The session was busy and Hermes slipped it into the running turn.
  steered,

  /// The session was busy and Hermes stopped the turn to answer this.
  redirected;

  static SubmitOutcome fromStatus(String? status) =>
      switch (status?.trim().toLowerCase()) {
        'queued' => queued,
        'steered' => steered,
        'redirected' => redirected,
        _ => started,
      };
}

/// One message of the prefix a branch is seeded with.
@immutable
final class SeedMessage {
  const SeedMessage(this.role, this.content);

  /// `user` or `assistant`.
  final String role;
  final String content;

  Map<String, Object?> toWire() => {'role': role, 'content': content};
}

/// A row of `session.active_list`: a live runtime session on the gateway.
@immutable
final class LiveStatus {
  const LiveStatus({
    required this.runtimeId,
    required this.storedId,
    required this.status,
  });

  final String runtimeId;
  final String storedId;

  /// `working`, `waiting`, `starting` or `idle`.
  final String status;

  /// A turn is running or a decision is pending. `starting` only means an
  /// agent is being built, which can happen without any prompt.
  bool get replying => status == 'working' || status == 'waiting';
  bool get waiting => status == 'waiting';
}

/// What a model can do beyond answering.
@immutable
final class ModelCaps {
  const ModelCaps({this.reasoning = false, this.fast = false});

  final bool reasoning;
  final bool fast;
}
```

In `ModelOptions`:
```dart
  /// What [model] can do on [provider], or null when the gateway did not say.
  ModelCaps? capsFor(String model, {String? provider}) {
    for (final candidate in providers) {
      if (provider != null && candidate.slug != provider) continue;
      final caps = candidate.capabilities[model];
      if (caps != null) return caps;
    }
    return null;
  }
```

4. Replace `parseInput` with:

```dart
  /// A sudo, secret or password-manager request. Null without a session,
  /// which nothing could answer.
  static InputRequest? parseInput(RpcServerRequest request) {
    final session = request.params['session_id'].string().trim();
    if (session.isEmpty || request.id.isEmpty) return null;
    String text(String key) => request.params[key].string().trim();
    return InputRequest(
      requestId: request.id,
      sessionId: session,
      kind: switch (request.method) {
        'sudo' => InputKind.sudo,
        'vault.unlock_prompt' => InputKind.vaultUnlock,
        'vault.code' => InputKind.vaultCode,
        _ => InputKind.secret,
      },
      command: text('command'),
      envVar: text('env_var'),
      prompt: text('prompt'),
      displayName: text('display_name'),
      site: text('site'),
      hint: text('hint'),
    );
  }

  /// A `vault.save_login` request. Null without a session or an origin.
  static LoginRequest? parseLogin(RpcServerRequest request) {
    final session = request.params['session_id'].string().trim();
    final origin = request.params['origin'].string().trim();
    if (session.isEmpty || origin.isEmpty || request.id.isEmpty) return null;
    return LoginRequest(
      requestId: request.id,
      sessionId: session,
      origin: origin,
      site: request.params['site'].string().trim(),
    );
  }

  /// The effort and fast mode a `session.info` reports, or null when it says
  /// neither. Separate from [fromEvent] because the same event reports the
  /// model.
  static SessionInfoChanged? infoFromEvent(RpcEvent event) {
    if (event.type != 'session.info') return null;
    final payload = event.payload;
    ReasoningEffort? effort;
    final raw = payload.any(const ['reasoning_effort', 'reasoning']);
    if (raw.isMap) {
      effort = raw['enabled'].boolOrNull == false
          ? ReasoningEffort.none
          : ReasoningEffort.fromWire(raw['effort'].stringOrNull);
    } else if (raw.hasValue) {
      effort = ReasoningEffort.fromWire(raw.stringOrNull);
    }
    final fastFlag = payload.any(const ['fast', 'fast_mode']).boolOrNull;
    final tier = payload['service_tier'].stringOrNull?.trim().toLowerCase();
    final fast = fastFlag ?? (tier == null ? null : tier == 'priority');
    if (effort == null && fast == null) return null;
    return SessionInfoChanged(
      event.sessionId ?? '',
      effort: effort,
      fast: fast,
    );
  }
```

6. Append the event classes at the end of the file:

```dart
/// The agent wants a username and password saved.
final class LoginAsked extends ChatEvent {
  LoginAsked(this.request) : super(request.sessionId);

  final LoginRequest request;
}

/// The agent opened a connection card.
final class ConnectionAsked extends ChatEvent {
  ConnectionAsked(this.request) : super(request.sessionId);

  final ConnectionRequest request;
}

/// A target of an open connection card changed, or the card settled.
final class ConnectionChanged extends ChatEvent {
  const ConnectionChanged(
    super.sessionId, {
    required this.opId,
    required this.targets,
    required this.settled,
  });

  final String opId;
  final List<ConnectionTarget> targets;
  final bool settled;
}

/// The reasoning effort and fast mode in force for the session.
final class SessionInfoChanged extends ChatEvent {
  const SessionInfoChanged(super.sessionId, {this.effort, this.fast});

  final ReasoningEffort? effort;
  final bool? fast;
}
```

(`copyWith` keeps the old value when an argument is null, which is exactly the "keep when absent" rule.)
6. Add the methods:

```dart
  ChatState withLogin(LoginRequest request) => copyWith(
    logins: [
      for (final login in logins)
        if (login.requestId != request.requestId) login,
      request,
    ],
    turn: TurnPhase.waiting,
  );

  ChatState withoutLogin(LoginRequest request) => _decided(
    copyWith(
      logins: [
        for (final login in logins)
          if (login.requestId != request.requestId) login,
      ],
    ),
  );

  ChatState withConnection(ConnectionRequest request) => copyWith(
    connections: [
      for (final open in connections)
        if (open.opId != request.opId) open,
      request,
    ],
    turn: TurnPhase.waiting,
  );

  ChatState withoutConnection(String opId) => _decided(
    copyWith(
      connections: [
        for (final open in connections)
          if (open.opId != opId) open,
      ],
    ),
  );

  /// An update for an open card merges in; a settled one closes it. An update
  /// for a card that is not open is ignored.
  ChatState withConnectionChange(ConnectionChanged change) {
    final open = connections.where((c) => c.opId == change.opId);
    if (open.isEmpty) return this;
    if (change.settled) return withoutConnection(change.opId);
    final updated = open.first.withTargets(change.targets, settled: false);
    return copyWith(
      connections: [
        for (final c in connections)
          if (c.opId == change.opId) updated else c,
      ],
    );
  }
```
7. Make `_waitingOr`, `_decided` and `_withdrawn` aware of the new lists:

- `_waitingOr`: `approvals.isNotEmpty || questions.isNotEmpty || inputs.isNotEmpty || logins.isNotEmpty || connections.isNotEmpty`.

1. Interface: change `submit` and add the new methods exactly as in the Interfaces block above (with doc comments in the existing style).

```dart
  /// Sends [text] as a turn, or, on a busy session, as Hermes's busy policy
  /// decides. [queued] forces "run after the current reply".
  Future<Result<SubmitOutcome>> submit(
    String runtimeId,
    String text, {
    bool queued = false,
  });

  /// Deletes [storedId] for good. Hermes refuses while it is live.
  Future<Result<void>> delete(String storedId);

  /// Removes the last user turn and everything after it. Returns how many
  /// messages went.
  Future<Result<int>> undo(String runtimeId);

  /// Copies all of [storedId] into a new conversation.
  Future<Result<NewConversation>> branchWhole(String storedId);

  /// A new conversation seeded with [prefix] (the visible user and assistant
  /// text up to a chosen reply), linked to [storedId] as its parent.
  Future<Result<NewConversation>> branchFrom(
    String storedId,
    List<SeedMessage> prefix, {
    required String title,
  });

  /// Summarises older turns in the live session.
  Future<Result<void>> compress(String runtimeId);

  /// What Hermes does with a message sent mid-reply, for this profile.
  Future<Result<BusyMode>> busyMode();
  Future<Result<void>> setBusyMode(BusyMode mode);

  Future<Result<void>> setReasoning(String runtimeId, ReasoningEffort effort);
  Future<Result<void>> setFast(String runtimeId, {required bool fast});

  /// Answers a save-login prompt. Both values go straight into the reply
  /// frame and are kept nowhere.
  Future<Result<void>> answerLogin(
    LoginRequest request, {
    required String identifier,
    required String password,
  });

  /// Answers [targets] of an open connection card; [settle] also closes it.
  Future<Result<void>> answerConnection(
    ConnectionRequest request,
    List<TargetAnswer> targets, {
    bool settle = false,
  });

  /// The runtime sessions live on the gateway right now.
  Future<Result<List<LiveStatus>>> activeSessions();
```

```dart
    String runtimeId,

  }) async => (await _session.call(

    params: {
      'session_id': runtimeId,
      'text': text,
      if (queued) 'queued': true,
    },

  @override
  Future<Result<void>> delete(String storedId) async =>
      (await _session.connection.client.delete(
        '/api/sessions/${Uri.encodeComponent(storedId)}',
        query: _scoped(const {}),
      )).map((_) {});

  @override
  Future<Result<int>> undo(String runtimeId) async => (await _session.call(
    'session.undo',
    params: {'session_id': runtimeId},
  )).map((json) => json['removed'].integer());

  @override
  Future<Result<NewConversation>> branchWhole(String storedId) async =>

        await _session.call(
          'session.branch_stored',
          params: _scoped({'parent_session_id': storedId, 'cols': 96, 'source': 'desktop'}),
        ),

      );

  @override
  Future<Result<NewConversation>> branchFrom(
    String storedId,
    List<SeedMessage> prefix, {
    required String title,
  }) async => _newConversation(
    await _session.call(
      'session.create',
      params: _scoped({
        'cols': 96,
        'source': 'desktop',
        'parent_session_id': storedId,
        'title': title,
        'messages': [for (final message in prefix) message.toWire()],
      }),
    ),
    endpoint: 'session.create',
  );

  @override
  Future<Result<void>> compress(String runtimeId) async =>
      (await _session.call(
        'session.compress',
        params: {'session_id': runtimeId},
        timeout: const Duration(seconds: 120),
      )).map((_) {});

  @override
  Future<Result<BusyMode>> busyMode() async => (await _session.call(
    'config.set',
    params: _scoped({'key': 'busy', 'value': 'status'}),
  )).map((json) => BusyMode.fromWire(json['value'].stringOrNull));

  @override
  Future<Result<void>> setBusyMode(BusyMode mode) async =>
      (await _session.call(
        'config.set',
        params: _scoped({'key': 'busy', 'value': mode.wire}),
      )).map((_) {});

  @override
  Future<Result<void>> setReasoning(
    String runtimeId,
    ReasoningEffort effort,
  ) async => (await _session.call(
    'config.set',
    params: {'key': 'reasoning', 'session_id': runtimeId, 'value': effort.wire},
  )).map((_) {});

  @override
  Future<Result<void>> setFast(String runtimeId, {required bool fast}) async =>
      (await _session.call(
        'config.set',
        params: {
          'key': 'fast',
          'session_id': runtimeId,
          'value': fast ? 'fast' : 'normal',
        },
      )).map((_) {});

  @override
  Future<Result<void>> answerLogin(
    LoginRequest request, {
    required String identifier,
    required String password,
  }) async =>
      // Straight into the reply frame; never logged or kept.
      _session.replyToServerRequest(request.requestId, {
        // Both empty is a skip, which Hermes reads as an empty value.
        'value': identifier.isEmpty && password.isEmpty
            ? ''
            : jsonEncode({'identifier': identifier, 'password': password}),
      })
      ? const Ok(null)
      : const Err(
          IrisFailure.offline(message: 'Not connected to the dashboard.'),
        );

  @override
  Future<Result<void>> answerConnection(
    ConnectionRequest request,
    List<TargetAnswer> targets, {
    bool settle = false,
  }) async => (await _session.call(
    'connection.respond',
    params: _scoped({
      'owner': {'type': 'session', 'session_id': request.sessionId},
      'op_id': request.opId,
      'result': {
        'targets': [for (final target in targets) target.toWire()],
        if (settle) 'settled_by': 'continue',
      },
    }),
  )).map((_) {});

  @override
  Future<Result<List<LiveStatus>>> activeSessions() async =>
      (await _session.call(
        'session.active_list',
        params: _scoped(const {}),
      )).map(
        (json) => [
          for (final row in json['sessions'].list)
            if (row['id'].stringOrNull case final String id
                when id.isNotEmpty)
              LiveStatus(
                runtimeId: id,
                storedId: row['session_key'].string(),
                status: row['status'].string('idle'),
              ),
        ],
      );

  /// A new conversation from a `session.create`-shaped answer.
  Result<NewConversation> _newConversation(
    Result<Json> result, {
    required String endpoint,
  }) {
    switch (result) {
      case Err(:final failure):
        return Err(failure);
      case Ok(value: final json):
        final runtimeId = HistoryParser.firstText(json, const ['session_id']);
        if (runtimeId == null) {
          return Err(
            IrisFailure.malformed(
              message: 'The dashboard did not return a session id.',
              endpoint: endpoint,
            ),
          );
        }
        return Ok(
          NewConversation(
            runtimeId: runtimeId,
            storedId:
                HistoryParser.firstText(json, const ['stored_session_id']) ??
                runtimeId,
          ),
        );
    }
  }
```
Make `create()` use `_newConversation(result, endpoint: 'session.create')` so the two cannot drift.

```dart
  @override
  Future<Result<SubmitOutcome>> submit(
    String runtimeId,
    String text, {
    bool queued = false,
  }) async {
    submitted.add((runtimeId, text));
```

and implement the rest, each recording then returning its scripted result:

```dart
  @override
  Future<Result<void>> delete(String storedId) async {
    deletes.add(storedId);
    return deleteResult;
  }

  @override
  Future<Result<int>> undo(String runtimeId) async {
    undos.add(runtimeId);
    return undoResult;
  }

  @override
  Future<Result<NewConversation>> branchWhole(String storedId) async {
    branches.add(storedId);
    branchPrefixes.add(const []);
    return branchResult;
  }

  @override
  Future<Result<NewConversation>> branchFrom(
    String storedId,
    List<SeedMessage> prefix, {
    required String title,
  }) async {
    branches.add(storedId);
    branchPrefixes.add(prefix);
    return branchResult;
  }

  @override
  Future<Result<void>> compress(String runtimeId) async {
    compressions.add(runtimeId);
    return compressResult;
  }

  @override
  Future<Result<BusyMode>> busyMode() async => busyModeResult;

  @override
  Future<Result<void>> setBusyMode(BusyMode mode) async {
    busyWrites.add(mode);
    return setBusyResult;
  }

  @override
  Future<Result<void>> setReasoning(String runtimeId, ReasoningEffort effort) async {
    efforts.add(effort);
    return effortResult;
  }

  @override
  Future<Result<void>> setFast(String runtimeId, {required bool fast}) async {
    fasts.add(fast);
    return fastResult;
  }

  @override
  Future<Result<void>> answerLogin(
    LoginRequest request, {
    required String identifier,
    required String password,
  }) async {
    logins.add((request.requestId, identifier, password));
    return loginResult;
  }

  @override
  Future<Result<void>> answerConnection(
    ConnectionRequest request,
    List<TargetAnswer> targets, {
    bool settle = false,
  }) async {
    connectionAnswers.add((request.opId, targets, settle));
    return connectionResult;
  }

  @override
  Future<Result<List<LiveStatus>>> activeSessions() async => activeResult;
```

```dart
import 'package:iris/data/prefs/json_list.dart';

void main() {
  test('round-trips a list', () {
    expect(decodeStringList(encodeStringList(['a', 'b'])), ['a', 'b']);
  });

  test('an empty list is stored as no value at all', () {
    expect(encodeStringList(const []), isNull);
  });

  test('damaged or missing values decode to empty, never throw', () {
    expect(decodeStringList(null), isEmpty);
    expect(decodeStringList(''), isEmpty);
    expect(decodeStringList('not json'), isEmpty);
    expect(decodeStringList('{"a":1}'), isEmpty);
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Create `lib/data/prefs/json_list.dart`**

```dart
/// Lists of strings in a preference value.
///
/// A preference is one string, so a list is JSON. Decoding never throws: a
/// damaged value is an empty list, the same rule as the usage selection.
library;

import 'dart:convert';

List<String> decodeStringList(String? raw) {
  if (raw == null || raw.isEmpty) return const [];
  try {
    final decoded = jsonDecode(raw);
    if (decoded is! List) return const [];
    final seen = <String>{};
    return [
      for (final entry in decoded)
        if (entry is String && entry.isNotEmpty && seen.add(entry)) entry,
    ];
  } on FormatException {
    return const [];
  }
}

/// The value to store, or null when there is nothing to store, which removes
/// the key.
String? encodeStringList(List<String> values) =>
    values.isEmpty ? null : jsonEncode(values);
```

```dart
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:iris/app/device_state.dart';
import 'package:iris/app/preferences.dart';

import 'package:iris/data/prefs/preferences_store.dart';

void main() {
  late MemoryPreferencesStore store;
  late ProviderContainer container;

  setUp(() {
    store = MemoryPreferencesStore();
    container = ProviderContainer(
      overrides: [preferencesStoreProvider.overrideWithValue(store)],
    );
    addTearDown(container.dispose);
    container.read(activeServerIdProvider.notifier).select('srv');
  });

  group('pins', () {
    test('pin puts the newest first and persists per server and profile', () {
      final pins = container.read(pinsProvider.notifier);
      pins.pin('a');
      pins.pin('b');
      expect(container.read(pinsProvider), ['b', 'a']);
      expect(store.values[('srv/default', PreferenceKeys.pins)], '["b","a"]');
    });

    test('pinning twice moves it to the front, unpinning removes it', () {
      final pins = container.read(pinsProvider.notifier)
        ..pin('a')
        ..pin('b')
        ..pin('a');
      expect(container.read(pinsProvider), ['a', 'b']);
      pins.unpin('a');
      expect(container.read(pinsProvider), ['b']);
      pins.unpin('b');
      expect(store.values.containsKey(('srv/default', PreferenceKeys.pins)), isFalse);
    });

    test('pins follow the profile', () {
      container.read(pinsProvider.notifier).pin('a');
      container.read(activeProfileProvider.notifier).select('ananta');
      expect(container.read(pinsProvider), isEmpty);
      container.read(pinsProvider.notifier).pin('z');
      container.read(activeProfileProvider.notifier).select('default');
      expect(container.read(pinsProvider), ['a']);
    });

    test('toggle pins then unpins', () {
      final pins = container.read(pinsProvider.notifier);
      pins.toggle('a');
      expect(pins.isPinned('a'), isTrue);
      pins.toggle('a');
      expect(pins.isPinned('a'), isFalse);
    });
  });

  group('recent searches', () {
    test('newest first, no duplicates ignoring case, at most six', () {
      final recent = container.read(recentSearchesProvider.notifier);
      for (final q in ['one', 'two', 'three', 'four', 'five', 'six', 'seven']) {
        recent.add(q);
      }
      expect(container.read(recentSearchesProvider), ['seven', 'six', 'five', 'four', 'three', 'two']);
      recent.add('SIX');
      expect(container.read(recentSearchesProvider).first, 'SIX');
      expect(container.read(recentSearchesProvider).where((q) => q.toLowerCase() == 'six'), hasLength(1));
    });

    test('blank queries are not remembered, and clear empties the list', () {
      final recent = container.read(recentSearchesProvider.notifier)
        ..add('   ')
        ..add('heartbeat');
      expect(container.read(recentSearchesProvider), ['heartbeat']);
      recent.clear();
      expect(container.read(recentSearchesProvider), isEmpty);
    });
  });

  group('image hosts', () {
    test('allow remembers a host, lower-cased, once', () {
      final hosts = container.read(imageHostsProvider.notifier)
        ..allow('Grafana.Local')
        ..allow('grafana.local');
      expect(container.read(imageHostsProvider), {'grafana.local'});
      expect(hosts.state, {'grafana.local'});
    });
  });

  group('branch records', () {
    test('save, read back and forget', () {
      final records = container.read(branchRecordsProvider);
      records.save('child', const BranchRecord(parentId: 'p', parentTitle: 'Parent', count: 4));
      expect(container.read(branchRecordProvider('child'))?.parentTitle, 'Parent');
      records.forget('child');
      expect(container.read(branchRecordProvider('child')), isNull);
    });

    test('a damaged record reads as none', () {
      container.read(preferencesProvider.notifier).set('srv/default', PreferenceKeys.branch('x'), 'not json');
      expect(container.read(branchRecordProvider('x')), isNull);
    });
  });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Create `lib/app/device_state.dart`**

```dart
/// Small lists the phone remembers for the person, none of which sync:
/// pinned conversations, where a branch came from, recent searches, and the
/// hosts whose Markdown images load without a tap.
///
/// Stored on the Preferences table like every other setting, and written
/// through the same controller, so a failed write is logged and never blocks
/// the screen that made it.
library;

import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

import '../data/prefs/json_list.dart';
import '../data/prefs/preferences_store.dart';
import 'preferences.dart';
import 'providers.dart';

/// `<server>/<profile>`: pins and branch links are per profile because a
/// conversation id belongs to one profile's database.
String? _profileScope(Ref ref) {
  final server = ref.watch(activeServerIdProvider);
  if (server == null) return null;
  return '$server/${ref.watch(activeProfileProvider)}';
}

final pinsProvider = NotifierProvider<Pins, List<String>>(Pins.new);

/// Pinned conversation ids, newest pin first.
class Pins extends Notifier<List<String>> {
  String? _scope;

  @override
  List<String> build() {
    _scope = _profileScope(ref);
    if (_scope == null) return const [];
    return decodeStringList(
      ref.read(preferencesProvider)[(_scope!, PreferenceKeys.pins)],
    );
  }

  bool isPinned(String id) => state.contains(id);

  void pin(String id) => _set([id, ...state.where((p) => p != id)]);

  void unpin(String id) => _set([...state.where((p) => p != id)]);

  void toggle(String id) => isPinned(id) ? unpin(id) : pin(id);

  void _set(List<String> next) {
    state = next;
    final scope = _scope;
    if (scope == null) return;
    ref
        .read(preferencesProvider.notifier)
        .set(scope, PreferenceKeys.pins, encodeStringList(next));
  }
}

final recentSearchesProvider =
    NotifierProvider<RecentSearches, List<String>>(RecentSearches.new);

/// The last few searches on this server, newest first.
class RecentSearches extends Notifier<List<String>> {
  static const limit = 6;

  String? _server;

  @override
  List<String> build() {
    _server = ref.watch(activeServerIdProvider);
    if (_server == null) return const [];
    return decodeStringList(
      ref.read(preferencesProvider)[(_server!, PreferenceKeys.searchRecent)],
    );
  }

  void add(String query) {
    final text = query.trim();
    if (text.isEmpty) return;
    final lowered = text.toLowerCase();
    _set(
      [
        text,
        ...state.where((q) => q.toLowerCase() != lowered),
      ].take(limit).toList(),
    );
  }

  void clear() => _set(const []);

  void _set(List<String> next) {
    state = next;
    final server = _server;
    if (server == null) return;
    ref
        .read(preferencesProvider.notifier)
        .set(server, PreferenceKeys.searchRecent, encodeStringList(next));
  }
}

final imageHostsProvider = NotifierProvider<ImageHosts, Set<String>>(
  ImageHosts.new,
);

/// Hosts the person said to load Markdown images from without asking.
class ImageHosts extends Notifier<Set<String>> {
  String? _server;

  @override
  Set<String> build() {
    _server = ref.watch(activeServerIdProvider);
    if (_server == null) return const {};
    return decodeStringList(
      ref.read(preferencesProvider)[(_server!, PreferenceKeys.imageHosts)],
    ).toSet();
  }

  void allow(String host) {
    final name = host.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (name.isEmpty || state.contains(name)) return;
    state = {...state, name};
    final server = _server;
    if (server == null) return;
    ref
        .read(preferencesProvider.notifier)
        .set(
          server,
          PreferenceKeys.imageHosts,
          encodeStringList(state.toList()),
        );
  }
}

/// Where a branch came from, kept so the new chat can say so after a
/// restart. Hermes's own `parent_session_id` also links context-compaction
/// chains, so it cannot tell a branch from a compaction.
@immutable
final class BranchRecord {
  const BranchRecord({
    required this.parentId,
    required this.parentTitle,
    required this.count,
  });

  /// Null for a value that is not a record.
  static BranchRecord? tryParse(String? raw) {
    if (raw == null) return null;
    try {
      final decoded = jsonDecode(raw);
      if (decoded is! Map) return null;
      final parent = decoded['parent'];
      if (parent is! String || parent.isEmpty) return null;
      final title = decoded['parentTitle'];
      final count = decoded['count'];
      return BranchRecord(
        parentId: parent,
        parentTitle: title is String ? title : '',
        count: count is int ? count : 0,
      );
    } on FormatException {
      return null;
    }
  }

  final String parentId;
  final String parentTitle;

  /// Messages copied from the parent.
  final int count;

  String toJson() => jsonEncode({
    'parent': parentId,
    'parentTitle': parentTitle,
    'count': count,
  });
}

/// The branch record for the conversation [childId] in the active profile.
final branchRecordProvider = Provider.family<BranchRecord?, String>((
  ref,
  childId,
) {
  final scope = _profileScope(ref);
  if (scope == null) return null;
  return BranchRecord.tryParse(
    ref.watch(preferencesProvider)[(scope, PreferenceKeys.branch(childId))],
  );
});

final branchRecordsProvider = Provider<BranchRecords>(BranchRecords.new);

/// Writes and removes branch records for the active profile.
final class BranchRecords {
  BranchRecords(this._ref);

  final Ref _ref;

  void save(String childId, BranchRecord record) {
    final scope = _profileScope(_ref);
    if (scope == null) return;
    _ref
        .read(preferencesProvider.notifier)
        .set(scope, PreferenceKeys.branch(childId), record.toJson());
  }

  void forget(String childId) {
    final scope = _profileScope(_ref);
    if (scope == null) return;
    _ref
        .read(preferencesProvider.notifier)
        .set(scope, PreferenceKeys.branch(childId), null);
  }
}
```

Note `_profileScope` uses `ref.watch`; inside `BranchRecords` (a plain class holding a `Ref`), use `ref.read` semantics instead. If the analyzer or runtime rejects `watch` outside a build, change `_profileScope` to take a `bool watch` flag (`watch ? ref.watch(x) : ref.read(x)`) and call it with `watch: false` from `BranchRecords`.

- [ ] **Step 3: `send_glyph.dart`**

```dart
/// The glyphs of the composer's one action: send, queue, steer, interrupt
/// and stop.
///
/// Drawn rather than taken from the icon font because the four busy-mode
/// glyphs must be told apart at a glance and must avoid two existing
/// meanings: steer must not look like "branch" (so it is one stroke with a
/// turn and no nodes) and interrupt must not look like "undo" (so it points
/// up and breaks a bar). All use a 2.4 stroke on a 24 grid.
library;

import 'package:flutter/widgets.dart';

import '../../data/chat/chat_models.dart';

enum SendKind {
  send('Send'),
  queue('Queue'),
  steer('Steer'),
  interrupt('Interrupt'),
  stop('Stop');

  const SendKind(this.semanticLabel);

  final String semanticLabel;

  static SendKind forMode(BusyMode mode) => switch (mode) {
    BusyMode.queue => queue,
    BusyMode.steer => steer,
    BusyMode.interrupt => interrupt,
  };
}

class SendGlyph extends StatelessWidget {
  const SendGlyph(this.kind, {required this.color, this.size = 24, super.key});

  final SendKind kind;
  final Color color;
  final double size;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => SizedBox(
    width: size,
    height: size,
    child: CustomPaint(painter: _GlyphPainter(kind, color)),
  );
}

class _GlyphPainter extends CustomPainter {
  const _GlyphPainter(this.kind, this.color);

  final SendKind kind;
  final Color color;

  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    final scale = size.width / 24;
    canvas.scale(scale);
    final line = Paint()
      ..color = color
      ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
      ..strokeWidth = 2.4
      ..strokeCap = StrokeCap.round
      ..strokeJoin = StrokeJoin.round;
    Path chevron(double x, double y, double reach) => Path()
      ..moveTo(x - reach, y + reach)
      ..lineTo(x, y)
      ..lineTo(x + reach, y + reach);
    switch (kind) {
      case SendKind.send:
        canvas
          ..drawLine(const Offset(12, 19), const Offset(12, 5), line)
          ..drawPath(chevron(12, 5, 6), line);
      case SendKind.queue:
        // An arrow onto a stack of two lines.
        canvas
          ..drawLine(const Offset(12, 13), const Offset(12, 4), line)
          ..drawPath(chevron(12, 4, 5), line)
          ..drawLine(const Offset(5, 17), const Offset(19, 17), line)
          ..drawLine(const Offset(7.5, 21), const Offset(16.5, 21), line);
      case SendKind.steer:
        // One stroke that bends and ends in an arrowhead.
        canvas
          ..drawPath(
            Path()
              ..moveTo(7, 20)
              ..lineTo(7, 12)
              ..quadraticBezierTo(7, 7, 12, 7)
              ..lineTo(18, 7),
            line,
          )
          ..drawPath(
            Path()
              ..moveTo(14.5, 3.5)
              ..lineTo(18, 7)
              ..lineTo(14.5, 10.5),
            line,
          );
      case SendKind.interrupt:
        // An arrow that points up and breaks a bar.
        canvas
          ..drawLine(const Offset(12, 16), const Offset(12, 4), line)
          ..drawPath(chevron(12, 4, 5.5), line)
          ..drawLine(const Offset(4, 20), const Offset(9, 20), line)
          ..drawLine(const Offset(15, 20), const Offset(20, 20), line);
      case SendKind.stop:
        canvas.drawRRect(
          RRect.fromRectAndRadius(
            const Rect.fromLTWH(6.5, 6.5, 11, 11),
            const Radius.circular(2.5),
          ),
          Paint()..color = color,
        );
    }
  }

  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(_GlyphPainter old) =>
      old.kind != kind || old.color != color;
}
```

```dart
/// The card every prompt that needs the person shares: the password
/// manager's unlock, code and save-login, sudo, and secrets.
///
/// One card, so the whole family reads as one thing: the needs-you colour
/// at 45% on the border, a mono eyebrow, the orb in the needs-you state. Once
/// answered, skipped or withdrawn it collapses to a [SettledLine] that never
/// shows what was typed.
library;

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../theme/iris_theme.dart';
import '../tokens/metrics.dart';
import '../tokens/palette.dart';
import 'iris_orb.dart';
import 'primitives.dart';

class NeedsYouCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const NeedsYouCard({
    required this.eyebrow,
    required this.title,
    required this.child,
    this.subtitle,
    this.actions,
    super.key,
  });

  /// Mono caps above the title, such as `Needs you · unlock`.
  final String eyebrow;
  final String title;
  final String? subtitle;

  /// The fields.
  final Widget child;

  /// The buttons, pinned under the fields.
  final Widget? actions;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final palette = context.palette;
    final type = context.type;
    final needs = palette.colorFor(AgentState.waiting);
    return DecoratedBox(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: palette.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
        border: Border.all(
          color: needs.withValues(alpha: .45),
          width: IrisStroke.hairline,
        ),
      ),
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            Row(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                const IrisOrb(state: AgentState.waiting, size: 28),
                const SizedBox(width: IrisSpace.md),
                Expanded(
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      IrisLabel(eyebrow, color: needs, small: true),
                      const SizedBox(height: 2),
                      Text(
                        title,
                        style: type.body.copyWith(
                          fontSize: 15.5,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                          color: palette.ink,
                        ),
                      ),
                      if (subtitle case final text?) ...[
                        const SizedBox(height: 2),
                        Text(
                          text,
                          style: type.body.copyWith(
                            fontSize: 13.5,
                            color: palette.inkMuted,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 14),
            child,
            if (actions case final buttons?) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: 14),
              buttons,
            ],
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

enum SettledOutcome { answered, skipped, expired }

/// What is left of a [NeedsYouCard] once it is over: one 56-high line. It
/// carries the title and a short detail ("answered · 14:02"), never a value.
class SettledLine extends StatelessWidget {
  const SettledLine({
    required this.title,
    required this.outcome,
    this.detail = '',
    super.key,
  });

  final String title;
  final SettledOutcome outcome;
  final String detail;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final palette = context.palette;
    final type = context.type;
    final tint = switch (outcome) {
      SettledOutcome.answered => palette.colorFor(AgentState.done),
      SettledOutcome.skipped => palette.colorFor(AgentState.rest),
      SettledOutcome.expired => palette.inkMuted,
    };
    final icon = switch (outcome) {
      SettledOutcome.answered => Icons.check,
      SettledOutcome.skipped => Icons.remove,
      SettledOutcome.expired => Icons.hourglass_empty,
    };
    final decoration = BoxDecoration(
      color: outcome == SettledOutcome.expired
          ? null
          : tint.withValues(alpha: .08),
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
      border: outcome == SettledOutcome.expired
          ? null
          : Border.all(color: tint.withValues(alpha: .25), width: IrisStroke.hairline),
    );
    final row = Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Icon(icon, size: 18, color: tint),
          const SizedBox(width: IrisSpace.md),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  title,
                  maxLines: 1,
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                  style: type.body.copyWith(fontSize: 14, color: palette.ink),
                ),
                if (detail.isNotEmpty)
                  Text(
                    detail,
                    maxLines: 1,
                    overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                    style: type.codeSmall.copyWith(
                      fontSize: 11,
                      color: palette.inkMuted,
                    ),
                  ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
    return Semantics(
      container: true,
      label: [title, if (detail.isNotEmpty) detail].join(', '),
      excludeSemantics: true,
      child: outcome == SettledOutcome.expired
          ? CustomPaint(
              painter: _DashedBorder(palette.lineStrong),
              child: ConstrainedBox(
                constraints: const BoxConstraints(minHeight: 56),
                child: Align(alignment: Alignment.centerLeft, child: row),
              ),
            )
          : DecoratedBox(
              decoration: decoration,
              child: ConstrainedBox(
                constraints: const BoxConstraints(minHeight: 56),
                child: Align(alignment: Alignment.centerLeft, child: row),
              ),
            ),
    );
  }
}

class _DashedBorder extends CustomPainter {
  const _DashedBorder(this.color);

  final Color color;

  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    final path = Path()
      ..addRRect(
        RRect.fromRectAndRadius(Offset.zero & size, const Radius.circular(18)),
      );
    final paint = Paint()
      ..color = color
      ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
      ..strokeWidth = 1;
    for (final metric in path.computeMetrics()) {
      var at = 0.0;
      while (at < metric.length) {
        canvas.drawPath(metric.extractPath(at, at + 5), paint);
        at += 9;
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(_DashedBorder old) => old.color != color;
}
```

```dart
      themed(Builder(builder: (context) => TextButton(
        onPressed: () async => answer = await showDeleteSheet(context, 'Weekly planning'),
        child: const Text('go'),
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `delete_sheet.dart`**

```dart
/// Asking before a conversation is deleted for good, and recognising
/// Hermes's refusal to delete one that is replying.
library;

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../core/failure.dart';
import '../../design/components/sheet.dart';
import '../../design/theme/iris_theme.dart';
import '../../design/tokens/metrics.dart';

/// True when the person confirms. Delete is the failed colour: it cannot be
/// undone.
Future<bool> showDeleteSheet(BuildContext context, String title) async {
  final confirmed = await showIrisSheet<bool>(
    context: context,
    builder: (sheetContext) {
      final palette = sheetContext.palette;
      final type = sheetContext.type;
      Widget button(String label, {required bool delete}) => Expanded(
        child: Semantics(
          button: true,
          label: label,
          excludeSemantics: true,
          child: GestureDetector(
            behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
            onTap: () => Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop(delete),
            child: Container(
              height: 48,
              alignment: Alignment.center,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: delete ? palette.danger : palette.surface,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                border: delete
                    ? null
                    : Border.all(color: palette.lineStrong, width: IrisStroke.hairline),
              ),
              child: Text(
                label,
                style: type.body.copyWith(
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                  color: delete ? palette.onDanger : palette.ink,
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      );
      return Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 22, 20, 20),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Text(
              'Delete ‘$title’?',
              style: type.body.copyWith(
                fontSize: 17,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                color: palette.ink,
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.xs),
            Text(
              'This removes it from Hermes for good.',
              style: type.body.copyWith(fontSize: 14, color: palette.inkMuted),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.lg),
            Row(
              children: [
                button('Cancel', delete: false),
                const SizedBox(width: IrisSpace.sm),
                button('Delete', delete: true),
              ],
            ),
          ],
        ),
      );
    },
  );
  return confirmed ?? false;
}

/// Whether [failure] is Hermes declining to delete a conversation that is
/// live (error 4023 over RPC, a conflict over REST). The wording varies by
/// version, so the message is matched too.
bool refusedAsLive(IrisFailure failure) {
  if (failure.statusCode == 409 || failure.statusCode == 423) return true;
  final text = (failure.message ?? '').toLowerCase();
  return text.contains('active session') ||
      text.contains('is active') ||
      text.contains('still running') ||
      text.contains('4023');
}
```
(`palette.danger` / `onDanger` exist in `palette.dart`; the delete button is the failed colour per the design.)

- [ ] **Step 5: Home**

In `_ConversationListScreenState._actions` add:

and the method:

```dart
  Future<void> _delete(
    ChatBackend chat,
    ConversationSummary conversation,
    String title,
  ) async {
    if (!await showDeleteSheet(context, title) || !mounted) return;
    unawaited(IrisHaptics.commit());
    setState(() => _gone.add(conversation.id));
    final result = await chat.delete(conversation.id);
    if (!mounted) return;
    switch (result) {
      case Ok():
        ref.read(pinsProvider.notifier).unpin(conversation.id);
        ref.read(branchRecordsProvider).forget(conversation.id);
        showIrisToast(context, message: 'Deleted ‘$title’');
      case Err(:final failure):
        setState(() => _gone.remove(conversation.id));
        showIrisToast(
          context,
          message: refusedAsLive(failure)
              ? 'Stop the reply before deleting this chat.'
              : 'Could not delete: ${ChatController.describe(failure)}',
          actionLabel: refusedAsLive(failure) ? 'Open' : null,
          onAction: refusedAsLive(failure)
              ? () => unawaited(_open(chat, conversation: conversation))
              : null,
        );
    }
  }
```
Imports: `../../app/device_state.dart`, `delete_sheet.dart`.

- [ ] **Step 3: The live provider**

In `providers.dart`:

```dart
/// How often home asks which conversations are replying.
final liveIntervalProvider = Provider<Duration>(
  (ref) => const Duration(seconds: 15),
);

/// Conversations replying right now, by stored id, from the gateway's
/// read-only `session.active_list`. Read when home opens and then on a timer
/// while it is on screen. A server without the call has nothing live: the
/// section just never shows.
final liveChatsProvider = StreamProvider.autoDispose<Map<String, LiveStatus>>((
  ref,
) async* {
  final chat = await ref.watch(activeChatProvider.future);
  if (chat == null) {
    yield const {};
    return;
  }
  final interval = ref.watch(liveIntervalProvider);
  while (true) {
    final rows = await chat.activeSessions();
    yield switch (rows) {
      Ok(:final value) => {
        for (final row in value)
          if (row.replying && row.storedId.isNotEmpty) row.storedId: row,
      },
      Err() => const {},
    };
    await Future<void>.delayed(interval);
  }
});
```
(The loop ends when the provider is disposed because the stream is cancelled. Under test the interval is overridden to an hour, so no timer runs.)

```dart
  final Map<String, ConversationSummary> _fetchedPins = {};
  final Set<String> _fetching = {};

  /// Pins whose chat is not in the loaded pages are read one by one, so a
  /// pin on an older chat still shows. A chat that no longer exists drops
  /// its pin.
  Future<void> _fetchMissingPins(ChatBackend chat, List<String> pins, Set<String> loaded) async {
    for (final id in pins) {
      if (loaded.contains(id) || _fetchedPins.containsKey(id) || !_fetching.add(id)) continue;
      switch (await chat.conversation(id)) {
        case Ok(:final value):
          if (mounted) setState(() => _fetchedPins[id] = value);
        case Err(:final failure) when failure.kind == FailureKind.notFound:
          if (mounted) ref.read(pinsProvider.notifier).unpin(id);
        case Err():
          break;
      }
    }
  }
```
This needs a way to read one conversation's summary. Add `Future<Result<ConversationSummary>> conversation(String storedId);` to the `ChatBackend` interface (doc: "One conversation's row, for a pin that is not in the loaded pages."), and:

```dart
  @override
  Future<Result<ConversationSummary>> conversation(String storedId) async {
    final path = '/api/sessions/${Uri.encodeComponent(storedId)}';
    final result = await _session.connection.client.get(
      path,
      query: _scoped(const {}),
    );
    switch (result) {
      case Err(:final failure):
        return Err(failure);
      case Ok(:final value):
        final row = HistoryParser.conversationRow(value);
        return row == null
            ? Err(
                IrisFailure.malformed(
                  message: 'The dashboard did not describe that conversation.',
                  endpoint: path,
                ),
              )
            : Ok(row);
    }
  }
```

2. Add:

```dart
  /// The id of the last turn the person started: where "the last exchange"
  /// begins. Steered turns are corrections inside a turn, not starts. Null
  /// when none is loaded.
  String? get lastExchangeStartId {
    for (final item in items.reversed) {
      if (item is UserTurn && !item.steered) return item.id;
    }
    return null;
  }

  /// The ids of the last exchange: its user turn and everything after.
  Set<String> get lastExchangeIds {
    final start = lastExchangeStartId;
    if (start == null) return const {};
    final at = items.indexWhere((i) => i.id == start);
    return {for (final item in items.sublist(at)) item.id};
  }

  ChatState withCollapsing(Set<String> ids) => copyWith(collapsing: ids);

  /// The transcript without its last exchange.
  ChatState withoutLastExchange() {
    final start = lastExchangeStartId;
    if (start == null) return this;
    final at = items.indexWhere((i) => i.id == start);
    return copyWith(items: items.sublist(0, at), collapsing: const {});
  }
```
Note `copyWith(items: …)` with an empty list is fine (the `??` only skips null).

The undo method:

```dart
  /// Takes back the last exchange and hands its prompt back for editing.
  Future<void> undo() async {
    final runtimeId = _runtimeId;
    void say(String message, {String? actionLabel, VoidCallback? onAction}) =>
        onToast?.call(message, actionLabel: actionLabel, onAction: onAction);
    if (runtimeId == null) {
      say('Nothing to undo');
      return;
    }
    if (value.running) {
      say('Stop the reply first to undo.', actionLabel: 'Stop', onAction: () => unawaited(stop()));
      return;
    }
    final result = await _backend.undo(runtimeId);
    if (_disposed) return;
    switch (result) {
      case Err(:final failure):
        final rpc = failure.rpcError;
        if (rpc?.isMethodNotFound ?? false) {
          say("Undo isn't supported on this server.");
        } else if (rpc?.code == 4009) {
          say('Stop the reply first to undo.', actionLabel: 'Stop', onAction: () => unawaited(stop()));
        } else {
          say('Could not undo: ${describe(failure)}');
        }
      case Ok(value: 0):
        say('Nothing to undo');
      case Ok():
        final start = value.lastExchangeStartId;
        if (start == null) {
          // The turn began before the page that is loaded: read it again.
          await _reloadNewest();
          say('Undid the last turn');
          return;
        }
        final turn = value.items.firstWhere((i) => i.id == start) as UserTurn;
        _update(value.withCollapsing(value.lastExchangeIds));
        if (collapseDelay > Duration.zero) await Future<void>.delayed(collapseDelay);
        if (_disposed) return;
        _update(value.withoutLastExchange());
        onPromptReturned?.call(turn.text);
        say(
          turn.attachments.isEmpty
              ? 'Undid the last turn'
              : 'Undid the last turn. Re-attach any files.',
        );
    }
  }
```
`stop()` is the existing controller method.

- [ ] **Step 6: The menu**

`lib/features/chat/message_menu.dart`:

```dart
/// The menu that opens on a long-press of a message: Copy, Select text,
/// Branch from here, Undo.
///
/// The pressed message is spotlit: a scrim covers everything but a hole
/// around it, so the message stays crisp and the menu sits right beside it.
/// The menu opens below the message, or above it when it would reach the
/// composer.
library;

import 'dart:ui';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../design/platform/platform_feel.dart';
import '../../design/theme/iris_theme.dart';
import '../../design/tokens/metrics.dart';

enum MessageAction {
  copy('Copy', Icons.copy_outlined),
  select('Select text', Icons.text_fields),
  branch('Branch from here', Icons.call_split),
  undo('Undo', Icons.undo);

  const MessageAction(this.label, this.icon);

  final String label;
  final IconData icon;
}

/// The chosen action, or null when the menu was dismissed.
Future<MessageAction?> showMessageMenu(
  BuildContext context, {
  required Rect anchor,
  required bool alignEnd,
  required List<MessageAction> actions,
}) {
  unawaited(IrisHaptics.selection());
  return showGeneralDialog<MessageAction>(
    context: context,
    barrierDismissible: true,
    barrierLabel: 'Close menu',
    barrierColor: const Color(0x00000000),
    transitionDuration: MediaQuery.disableAnimationsOf(context)
        ? Duration.zero
        : const Duration(milliseconds: 160),
    pageBuilder: (dialogContext, _, _) => _MenuPage(
      anchor: anchor,
      alignEnd: alignEnd,
      actions: actions,
    ),
    transitionBuilder: (context, animation, _, child) =>
        FadeTransition(opacity: animation, child: child),
  );
}

class _MenuPage extends StatelessWidget {
  const _MenuPage({
    required this.anchor,
    required this.alignEnd,
    required this.actions,
  });

  final Rect anchor;
  final bool alignEnd;
  final List<MessageAction> actions;

  static const _width = 220.0;
  static const _rowHeight = 48.0;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final palette = context.palette;
    final type = context.type;
    final size = MediaQuery.sizeOf(context);
    final height = actions.length * _rowHeight;
    // The composer and keyboard take the bottom of the screen.
    final limit = size.height - MediaQuery.viewInsetsOf(context).bottom - 160;
    final below = anchor.bottom + 8;
    final top = below + height > limit
        ? (anchor.top - 8 - height).clamp(MediaQuery.paddingOf(context).top, limit)
        : below;
    final left = alignEnd
        ? (anchor.right - _width).clamp(IrisSpace.lg, size.width - _width - IrisSpace.lg)
        : anchor.left.clamp(IrisSpace.lg, size.width - _width - IrisSpace.lg);
    return Stack(
      children: [
        Positioned.fill(
          child: GestureDetector(
            behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
            onTap: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(),
            child: BackdropFilter(
              filter: ImageFilter.blur(sigmaX: 4, sigmaY: 4),
              child: CustomPaint(
                painter: _Spotlight(
                  hole: anchor.inflate(4),
                  scrim: palette.surfaceSunken.withValues(alpha: .72),
                  edge: palette.lineStrong,
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
        Positioned(
          left: left,
          top: top,
          width: _width,
          child: Material(
            type: MaterialType.transparency,
            child: DecoratedBox(
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: palette.surfaceRaised,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
                border: Border.all(color: palette.lineStrong, width: IrisStroke.hairline),
              ),
              child: Column(
                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                children: [
                  for (final action in actions)
                    InkWell(
                      onTap: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(action),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
                      child: SizedBox(
                        height: _rowHeight,
                        child: Padding(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
                          child: Row(
                            children: [
                              Icon(action.icon, size: 18, color: palette.ink),
                              const SizedBox(width: IrisSpace.md),
                              Text(
                                action.label,
                                style: type.body.copyWith(fontSize: 15, color: palette.ink),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

/// A scrim with a rounded hole around the pressed message.
class _Spotlight extends CustomPainter {
  const _Spotlight({required this.hole, required this.scrim, required this.edge});

  final Rect hole;
  final Color scrim;
  final Color edge;

  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    final rrect = RRect.fromRectAndRadius(hole, const Radius.circular(18));
    final path = Path.combine(
      PathOperation.difference,
      Path()..addRect(Offset.zero & size),
      Path()..addRRect(rrect),
    );
    canvas
      ..drawPath(path, Paint()..color = scrim)
      ..drawRRect(
        rrect,
        Paint()
          ..color = edge
          ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
          ..strokeWidth = 1,
      );
  }

  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(_Spotlight old) => old.hole != hole || old.scrim != scrim;
}
```
