```dart
/// The last exchange folds away: height to 0, scaled to .96, faded by 60% of
/// the way, blurred a pixel (frame A3). Under reduced motion it only fades.
class _Collapse extends StatelessWidget {
  const _Collapse({required this.child});

  final Widget child;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (context.reduceMotion) {
      return TweenAnimationBuilder<double>(
        tween: Tween(begin: 1, end: 0),
        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 150),
        builder: (context, t, child) => Opacity(opacity: t, child: child),
        child: child,
      );
    }
    return TweenAnimationBuilder<double>(
      tween: Tween(begin: 1, end: 0),
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
      curve: IrisCurves.exit,
      builder: (context, t, child) => ClipRect(
        child: Align(
          alignment: Alignment.bottomCenter,
          heightFactor: t,
          child: Opacity(
            opacity: (t / .4).clamp(0.0, 1.0),
            child: Transform.scale(scale: .96 + .04 * t, child: child),
          ),
        ),
      ),
      child: child,
    );
  }
}
```

3. `_hold`:

```dart
  Future<void> _hold(ChatState state, TranscriptItem item, Rect anchor) async {
    final isUser = item is UserTurn;
    final text = switch (item) {
      UserTurn(:final text) => text,
      AssistantTurn(:final text) => text,
      _ => '',
    };
    if (text.trim().isEmpty) return;
    final actions = [
      MessageAction.copy,
      MessageAction.select,
      if (isUser && item.id == state.lastExchangeStartId && !state.running && _chat.isLive)
        MessageAction.undo,
    ];
    final chosen = await showMessageMenu(context, anchor: anchor, alignEnd: isUser, actions: actions);
    if (!mounted) return;
    switch (chosen) {
      case MessageAction.copy:
        await Clipboard.setData(ClipboardData(text: text));
        if (mounted) _toast('Copied');
      case MessageAction.select:
        await showSelectableText(context, text);
      case MessageAction.undo:
        await _chat.undo();
      case MessageAction.branch || null:
        break;
    }
  }
```
(`Branch` is added to `actions` in Task 7. `Clipboard` is from `package:flutter/services.dart`.)

```dart
final class BranchOutcome {

    required this.count,
    required this.summarised,
```

On `ChatController`: `VoidCallback? onBranchRequested;` and

```dart
  /// The user and assistant text in [items], oldest first. Tool calls,
  /// notices and empty turns are left out: they are not conversation.
  static List<SeedMessage> seedFrom(List<TranscriptItem> items) => [
    for (final item in items)
      if (item is UserTurn && item.text.trim().isNotEmpty)
        SeedMessage('user', item.text)
      else if (item is AssistantTurn && item.text.trim().isNotEmpty)
        SeedMessage('assistant', item.text),
  ];

  /// Whether a branch can start at [itemId]: the chat is stored, and that
  /// reply is finished.
  bool canBranchFrom(String itemId) {
    if (_storedId == null) return false;
    final item = value.items.where((i) => i.id == itemId);
    if (item.isEmpty) return false;
    final it = item.first;
    return it is AssistantTurn && !it.streaming;
  }

  /// The prefix up to and including [itemId], reading earlier pages first
  /// so it is whole. Null when [itemId] is not in this conversation.
  Future<List<SeedMessage>?> prefixThrough(String itemId) async {
    var pages = 0;
    while (value.earlier != null && pages++ < 60) {
      await loadEarlier();
      if (_disposed) return null;
    }
    final at = value.items.indexWhere((i) => i.id == itemId);
    if (at < 0) return null;
    return seedFrom(value.items.sublist(0, at + 1));
  }

  /// Copies this conversation into a new one: all of it, or up to the reply
  /// [fromItemId]. With [summarise], the copy is compressed too.
  Future<Result<BranchOutcome>> branch({
    String? fromItemId,
    bool summarise = false,
  }) async {
    final storedId = _storedId;
    if (storedId == null) {
      return const Err(IrisFailure(FailureKind.rejected, message: 'Send a message first: there is nothing to branch yet.'));
    }
    final lastReply = value.items.whereType<AssistantTurn>().isEmpty
        ? null
        : value.items.whereType<AssistantTurn>().last.id;
    final whole = fromItemId == null || fromItemId == lastReply;
    final List<SeedMessage>? prefix;
    if (whole) {
      prefix = null;
    } else {
      prefix = await prefixThrough(fromItemId);
      if (prefix == null) {
        return const Err(IrisFailure(FailureKind.rejected, message: "That message isn't in this chat any more."));
      }
    }
    final result = whole
        ? await _backend.branchWhole(storedId)
        : await _backend.branchFrom(
            storedId,
            prefix!,
            title: 'Branch of ${value.title ?? 'conversation'}',
          );
    switch (result) {
      case Err(:final failure):
        return Err(
          (failure.rpcError?.isMethodNotFound ?? false)
              ? const IrisFailure(FailureKind.rejected, message: "This server can't branch conversations.")
              : failure,
        );
      case Ok(value: final created):
        var summarised = false;
        if (summarise) {
          summarised = (await _backend.compress(created.runtimeId)).isOk;
        }
        return Ok(
          BranchOutcome(
            conversation: created,
            count: (prefix ?? seedFrom(value.items)).length,
            summarised: summarised,
          ),
        );
    }
  }
```
(`Result` has `isErr`? If only `isOk` exists, use `!result.isOk` in the test.) In `send`, after the `/undo` line: `if (trimmed == '/branch') { onBranchRequested?.call(); return; }`.

```dart
        if (++seen == record.count) return i;
```

```dart
/// Shows "Branching…" over everything while [work] runs, then closes. The
/// orb is in the connecting state, as the design shows.
Future<T> withBranchingOverlay<T>(BuildContext context, String title, Future<T> work) async {
  final navigator = Navigator.of(context, rootNavigator: true);
  unawaited(
    showGeneralDialog<void>(
      context: context,
      barrierDismissible: false,
      barrierColor: context.palette.surfaceSunken.withValues(alpha: .86),
      pageBuilder: (_, _, _) => PopScope(
        canPop: false,
        child: Center(
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              const IrisOrb(state: AgentState.loading, size: 72),
              const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.lg),
              Material(
                type: MaterialType.transparency,
                child: Column(
                  children: [
                    IrisLabel('Branching…', color: context.palette.colorFor(AgentState.loading)),
                    const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.xs),
                    SizedBox(
                      width: 260,
                      child: Text(
                        'Copying ‘$title’ into a new chat. It opens as soon as Hermes has it.',
                        textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                        style: context.type.body.copyWith(color: context.palette.inkMuted),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    ),
  );
  try {
    return await work;
  } finally {
    if (navigator.canPop()) navigator.pop();
  }
}
```
(Imports: `iris_orb.dart`, `palette.dart`, `dart:async`.)

### Task 8: Sending while a reply runs — the busy policy, the queue tray, steer and interrupt

**Model: opus.** This task is a state machine with races (turn end, reconnect, failure). Read the whole task before starting.

**Files:**
- Create: `lib/features/chat/queue_tray.dart`, `lib/features/chat/busy_setting.dart`, `lib/design/components/dashed_border.dart`
- Modify: `lib/data/chat/chat_models.dart` (`UserTurn.copyWith`, `QueuedMessage`), `lib/features/chat/chat_state.dart`, `lib/features/chat/chat_controller.dart`, `lib/features/chat/composer.dart`, `lib/features/chat/chat_screen.dart`, `lib/features/chat/transcript_view.dart`, `lib/features/settings/settings_screen.dart`
- Test: `test/features/busy_policy_test.dart`, `test/features/queue_tray_test.dart`, `test/features/busy_setting_test.dart`; extend `test/features/chat_state_test.dart`, `test/features/chat_widgets_test.dart` (composer), `test/features/chat_screen_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ChatBackend.submit(…, queued:) → SubmitOutcome`, `busyMode()`, `setBusyMode()`, `steer()` (existing), `SendGlyph`/`SendKind` (Task 3), `ChatController.onToast` (Task 6), `UserTurn.steerPending/steerStep/fromQueue`, `AssistantTurn.redirected` (Task 6 Step 3).
- Produces:

```dart
final class QueuedMessage { const QueuedMessage({required this.id, required this.text}); final String id; final String text; }
// ChatState
List<QueuedMessage> queue; bool queuePaused; BusyMode busyMode;
ChatState withQueued(String id, String text); ChatState withoutQueued(String id);
ChatState withSteer(String text)              // now sets steerPending
ChatState withRedirect(String text)           // marks the live reply redirected, adds the user turn, turn → thinking
ChatState withUserTurn(String text, {List<String> attachments, bool fromQueue})
// ChatController
void removeQueued(String id); void clearQueue(); Future<void> sendNextQueued();
// busy_setting.dart
final busyModeProvider = FutureProvider.autoDispose<BusyMode?>(…);
Future<BusyMode?> showBusyPicker(BuildContext, {required BusyMode current, required String profile, required String host});
class QueueTray extends StatefulWidget { const QueueTray({required this.items, required this.paused, required this.onRemove, required this.onSendNext, required this.onClear}); }
```

Frames A4 (tray, glyphs, tag, toasts, slash list), A5 (setting), B1 (steer bubble).

The rules (spec "Sending while running"):

| Setting | Plain text while a turn runs |
|---|---|
| Queue | Into the tray, in memory. Nothing is sent. |
| Steer | `session.steer` (today's path). Bubble tagged "Waiting for next step", then "Picked up at step n". |
| Interrupt | `prompt.submit`; Hermes applies its policy. `redirected` drops the cut reply to muted ("stopped · redirected"). |

`/queue text` always queues while running and is a normal send when idle. `/steer text` always steers while running and is a normal send when idle. The tray drains oldest-first when a turn ends **normally**; a failed or stopped turn **pauses** it ("Send next" / "Clear"). A reconnect that finds the turn finished drains exactly one message.

5. New:

```dart
  /// The person redirected the running turn: Hermes stopped it to answer
  /// this. What was said so far stays, marked as cut off.
  ChatState withRedirect(String text) {
    final settled = _settled(items);
    final last = settled.isEmpty ? null : settled.last;
    final marked = last is AssistantTurn
        ? [...settled.sublist(0, settled.length - 1), last.copyWith(redirected: true)]
        : settled;
    return copyWith(
      items: [...marked, UserTurn(id: 'local-${items.length}', text: text)],
      turn: TurnPhase.thinking,
    );
  }
```
6. In `apply`, the `ToolStarted` case appends the call and then picks up pending steers:

```dart
    ToolStarted(:final toolId, :final name, :final input) => copyWith(
      items: _pickedUp([
        ..._settled(items),
        ToolCall(
          id: toolId ?? 'tool-${items.length}',
          name: name,
          arguments: input,
          status: ToolStatus.running,
        ),
      ]),
      turn: TurnPhase.working,
    ),
```
```dart
  /// Steers waiting for a step are picked up by the tool that just started:
  /// its position among this turn's tools is the step.
  static List<TranscriptItem> _pickedUp(List<TranscriptItem> items) {
    var tools = 0;
    for (final item in items.reversed) {
      if (item is UserTurn && !item.steered) break;
      if (item is ToolCall) tools++;
    }
    return [
      for (final item in items)
        if (item is UserTurn && item.steerPending)
          item.copyWith(steerPending: false, steerStep: tools)
        else
          item,
    ];
  }
```

```dart
/// What sending does while a reply is running, for each busy mode, and how
/// the queue tray drains.
library;

import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:iris/core/failure.dart';
import 'package:iris/core/result.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/chat/chat_event.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/chat/chat_models.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/rpc/rpc_frame.dart';
import 'package:iris/features/chat/chat_controller.dart';
import 'package:iris/features/chat/chat_state.dart';

import '../support/fake_chat_backend.dart';

void main() {
  late FakeChatBackend backend;
  late List<String> toasts;

  setUp(() {
    backend = FakeChatBackend();
    toasts = [];
  });
  tearDown(() => backend.close());

  Future<void> settle() => Future<void>.delayed(Duration.zero);

  /// An open conversation with a reply running.
  Future<ChatController> running({BusyMode mode = BusyMode.interrupt}) async {
    backend.busyModeResult = Ok(mode);
    final controller = ChatController(backend, conversationId: 'stored-1')
      ..onToast = (message, {actionLabel, onAction}) => toasts.add(message);
    addTearDown(controller.dispose);
    await controller.open();
    await settle();
    backend.emit(const MessageStarted('run-1'));
    await settle();
    expect(controller.value.running, isTrue);
    return controller;
  }

  Future<void> finish() async {
    backend.emit(const MessageCompleted('run-1', content: 'done'));
    await settle();
  }

  group('by mode', () {
    test('the mode is read when the chat opens', () async {
      final controller = await running(mode: BusyMode.queue);
      expect(controller.value.busyMode, BusyMode.queue);
    });

    test('a server without the setting means interrupt', () async {
      backend.busyModeResult = Err(unknownMethod('config.set'));
      final controller = ChatController(backend, conversationId: 'stored-1');
      addTearDown(controller.dispose);
      await controller.open();
      await settle();
      expect(controller.value.busyMode, BusyMode.interrupt);
    });

    test('queue mode puts text in the tray and sends nothing', () async {
      final controller = await running(mode: BusyMode.queue);
      await controller.send('Also check node-4');
      expect(backend.submits, isEmpty);
      expect(backend.steers, isEmpty);
      expect(controller.value.queue.single.text, 'Also check node-4');
      expect(toasts, ['Queued for after this reply']);
    });

    test('steer mode steers through session.steer', () async {
      final controller = await running(mode: BusyMode.steer);
      await controller.send('Skip staging');
      expect(backend.steers, [('run-1', 'Skip staging')]);
      expect(backend.submits, isEmpty);
      final turn = controller.value.items.whereType<UserTurn>().last;
      expect(turn.steered && turn.steerPending, isTrue);
      expect(toasts, ['Steered']);
    });

    test('interrupt mode submits, and a redirect cuts the reply', () async {
      backend.submitOutcome = const Ok(SubmitOutcome.redirected);
      final controller = await running();
      backend.emit(const TextDelta('run-1', 'Three things broke'));
      await settle();
      await controller.send('Just restart the worker');
      expect(backend.submits, [('run-1', 'Just restart the worker', false)]);
      expect(controller.value.items.whereType<AssistantTurn>().single.redirected, isTrue);
      expect(toasts, ['Redirected']);
    });

    test('interrupt mode: a gateway that says queued is accepted, not an error', () async {
      backend.submitOutcome = const Ok(SubmitOutcome.queued);
      final controller = await running();
      await controller.send('later');
      expect(toasts, ['Queued for after this reply']);
      expect(controller.value.items.whereType<Notice>().where((n) => n.isError), isEmpty);
    });

    test('a 4009 on an interrupt falls back to the steer path', () async {
      backend.submitResult = const Err(IrisFailure(
        FailureKind.rejected,
        message: 'session busy',
        cause: RpcError(code: 4009, message: 'session busy'),
      ));
      final controller = await running();
      await controller.send('nudge');
      expect(backend.steers, [('run-1', 'nudge')]);
    });

    test('other slash commands wait while running', () async {
      final controller = await running();
      await controller.send('/model');
      expect(backend.slashes, isEmpty);
      expect(backend.submits, isEmpty);
    });
  });

  group('slash forms', () {
    test('/queue always queues while running, whatever the mode', () async {
      final controller = await running(mode: BusyMode.steer);
      await controller.send('/queue after this');
      expect(controller.value.queue.single.text, 'after this');
      expect(backend.steers, isEmpty);

    test('/steer always steers while running, whatever the mode', () async {

    test('idle, /queue and /steer are ordinary sends of their argument', () async {
      final controller = ChatController(backend, conversationId: 'stored-1');
      addTearDown(controller.dispose);

  group('the tray', () {
    test('drains oldest first, one per finished turn', () async {
      final controller = await running(mode: BusyMode.queue);
      await controller.send('one');
      await controller.send('two');
      await finish();
      expect(backend.submits.map((s) => s.$2), ['one']);
      expect(controller.value.queue.map((q) => q.text), ['two']);
      final sent = controller.value.items.whereType<UserTurn>().last;
      expect(sent.text, 'one');
      expect(sent.fromQueue, isTrue);
      expect(controller.value.running, isTrue, reason: 'the next turn is under way');

      await finish();
      expect(backend.submits.map((s) => s.$2), ['one', 'two']);
      expect(controller.value.queue, isEmpty);
    });

    test('a failed turn pauses the tray; Send next resumes it; Clear empties it', () async {
      final controller = await running(mode: BusyMode.queue);
      await controller.send('one');
      await controller.send('two');
      backend.emit(const TurnFailed('run-1', 'boom'));
      await settle();
      expect(backend.submits, isEmpty);
      expect(controller.value.queuePaused, isTrue);

      await controller.sendNextQueued();
      expect(backend.submits.map((s) => s.$2), ['one']);
      expect(controller.value.queuePaused, isFalse);

      backend.emit(const TurnFailed('run-1', 'boom again'));
      await settle();
      expect(controller.value.queuePaused, isTrue);
      controller.clearQueue();
      expect(controller.value.queue, isEmpty);
      expect(controller.value.queuePaused, isFalse);
    });

    test('Stop pauses the tray rather than sending into a stopped turn', () async {
      final controller = await running(mode: BusyMode.queue);
      await controller.send('one');
      backend.emit(const TurnInterrupted('run-1'));
      await settle();
      expect(backend.submits, isEmpty);
      expect(controller.value.queuePaused, isTrue);
    });

    test('one message can be removed', () async {
      final controller = await running(mode: BusyMode.queue);
      await controller.send('one');
      await controller.send('two');
      controller.removeQueued(controller.value.queue.first.id);
      expect(controller.value.queue.map((q) => q.text), ['two']);
    });

    test('a reconnect that finds the turn finished sends exactly one message', () async {
      final controller = await running(mode: BusyMode.queue);
      await controller.send('one');
      await controller.send('two');
      backend.resumeResult = const Ok(ResumedConversation(runtimeId: 'run-2'));
      backend.reconnect();
      await settle();
      await settle();
      expect(backend.submits.map((s) => s.$2), ['one']);
      expect(controller.value.queue.map((q) => q.text), ['two']);
    });

    test('a reconnect with the turn still running sends nothing', () async {
      final controller = await running(mode: BusyMode.queue);
      await controller.send('one');
      backend.resumeResult = const Ok(ResumedConversation(runtimeId: 'run-2', running: true));
      backend.reconnect();
      await settle();
      await settle();
      expect(backend.submits, isEmpty);
    });
  });
}
```


2. In `open()`, after a successful resume: `unawaited(_loadBusyMode());`

```dart

```
(A failure — including a server without the setting — leaves the default, interrupt.)
3. Rewrite the top of `send` (keep the `/undo`, `/branch` lines from Tasks 6–7 first):

```dart
    final command = _busyCommand.firstMatch(trimmed);
    if (value.running) {
      await _sendWhileRunning(trimmed, command);
      return;
    }

```
and use `body` instead of `trimmed` for the rest of the idle path (the `withUserTurn`, the slash check `attachments.isEmpty && body.startsWith('/')`, and `promptWithReferences(body, references)`). Delete the old `if (value.running) { if (!trimmed.startsWith('/')) await _steer(trimmed); return; }` block.
4. New methods:

```dart
  Future<void> _sendWhileRunning(String text, RegExpMatch? command) async {
    if (command != null) {
      final argument = command.group(2)!.trim();
      if (command.group(1) == 'queue') {
        _enqueue(argument);
      } else {
        await _steer(argument);
      }
      return;
    }
    // Any other command waits for the turn to end.
    if (text.startsWith('/')) return;
    switch (value.busyMode) {
      case BusyMode.queue:
        _enqueue(text);
      case BusyMode.steer:
        await _steer(text);
      case BusyMode.interrupt:
        await _interruptWith(text);
    }
  }

  void _enqueue(String text) {
    _update(value.withQueued('queued-${_queueSerial++}', text));
    onToast?.call('Queued for after this reply');
  }

  /// Interrupt mode: Hermes applies its own policy to the submit, and says
  /// what it did.
  Future<void> _interruptWith(String text) async {
    final runtimeId = _runtimeId;
    if (runtimeId == null) return;
    final result = await _backend.submit(runtimeId, text);
    if (_disposed) return;
    switch (result) {
      case Ok(value: SubmitOutcome.redirected):
        _update(value.withRedirect(text));
        onToast?.call('Redirected');
      case Ok(value: SubmitOutcome.steered):
        _update(value.withSteer(text));
        onToast?.call('Steered');
      case Ok(value: SubmitOutcome.queued):
        // Hermes held it for after the turn. Accepted, not an error.
        onToast?.call('Queued for after this reply');
      case Ok():
        // The turn had already ended: this started a new one.
        _update(value.withUserTurn(text));
      case Err(:final failure):
        if (failure.rpcError?.code == 4009) {
          await _steer(text);
        } else {
          onSteerReturned?.call(text);
          onToast?.call('Could not send: ${describe(failure)}');
        }
    }
  }

  /// The tray's controls.
  void removeQueued(String id) => _update(value.withoutQueued(id));

  void clearQueue() => _update(value.copyWith(queue: const [], queuePaused: false));

  /// Sends the next queued message now, after a failed or stopped turn
  /// paused the tray.
  Future<void> sendNextQueued() async {
    _update(value.copyWith(queuePaused: false));
    await _drain();
  }

  /// Sends the oldest queued message as a new turn. The turn is marked
  /// running at once, so a second "turn ended" can never send a second
  /// message before this one has finished.
  Future<void> _drain() async {
    if (_draining || value.queue.isEmpty || value.queuePaused) return;
    final runtimeId = _runtimeId;
    if (runtimeId == null) return;
    _draining = true;
    final next = value.queue.first;
    _update(value.withoutQueued(next.id).withUserTurn(next.text, fromQueue: true));
    await _submit(runtimeId, next.text);
    _draining = false;
  }

  void _turnEnded({required bool failedOrStopped}) {
    if (value.queue.isEmpty) return;
    if (failedOrStopped) {
      _update(value.copyWith(queuePaused: true));
    } else {
      unawaited(_drain());
    }
  }
```
5. `_onEvent`:

```dart
  void _onEvent(ChatEvent event) {
    final id = event.sessionId;
    if (id.isEmpty || (id != _runtimeId && id != _storedId)) return;
    final wasRunning = value.running;
    _update(value.apply(event));
    if (_reloadWhenIdle && !value.running) unawaited(_reloadNewest());
    if (wasRunning && !value.running) {
      _turnEnded(failedOrStopped: event is TurnFailed || event is TurnInterrupted);
    }
  }
```
6. `_reattach`: capture `final wasRunning = value.running;` before the resume, and after `_update(next)` in the `Ok` branch add `if (wasRunning && !value.running) _turnEnded(failedOrStopped: false);`. (The queue is not reset by that `copyWith`.)
7. `_steer`: on `Ok(value: SteerOutcome.accepted)` also `onToast?.call('Steered');`.

- [ ] **Step 10: `dashed_border.dart`**

```dart
/// A dashed rounded border, for things that are pending or provisional: a
/// steer waiting for its step, an expired prompt.
library;

import 'package:flutter/widgets.dart';

class DashedRRectPainter extends CustomPainter {
  const DashedRRectPainter({
    required this.color,
    required this.radius,
    this.width = 1,
    this.dash = 5,
    this.gap = 4,
  });

  final Color color;
  final BorderRadius radius;
  final double width;
  final double dash;
  final double gap;

  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    final path = Path()..addRRect(radius.toRRect(Offset.zero & size));
    final paint = Paint()
      ..color = color
      ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
      ..strokeWidth = width;
    for (final metric in path.computeMetrics()) {
      var at = 0.0;
      while (at < metric.length) {
        canvas.drawPath(metric.extractPath(at, at + dash), paint);
        at += dash + gap;
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(DashedRRectPainter old) =>
      old.color != color || old.radius != radius || old.width != width;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 11: `queue_tray.dart`**

```dart
/// The messages waiting for the running reply to finish.
///
/// Pinned just above the composer rather than in the transcript: a reply
/// takes minutes and keeps growing, so a bubble at the bottom of the
/// transcript would keep sliding out of reach and sit between the streaming
/// reply and the person. The tray stays put, shows the order, and removes a
/// message in one tap. Four or more show the first two and a "+N more" row.
library;

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../data/chat/chat_models.dart';
import '../../design/components/primitives.dart';
import '../../design/theme/iris_theme.dart';
import '../../design/tokens/metrics.dart';

class QueueTray extends StatefulWidget {
  const QueueTray({
    required this.items,
    required this.paused,
    required this.onRemove,
    required this.onSendNext,
    required this.onClear,
    super.key,
  });

  final List<QueuedMessage> items;

  /// A failed or stopped turn paused the tray: nothing sends until the
  /// person says.
  final bool paused;
  final ValueChanged<String> onRemove;
  final VoidCallback onSendNext;
  final VoidCallback onClear;

  @override
  State<QueueTray> createState() => _QueueTrayState();
}

class _QueueTrayState extends State<QueueTray> {
  bool _expanded = false;

  static const _collapsedLimit = 3;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final palette = context.palette;
    final type = context.type;
    final items = widget.items;
    final collapse = items.length > _collapsedLimit && !_expanded;
    final shown = collapse ? items.take(2).toList() : items;
    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 6),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: palette.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
        border: Border.all(color: palette.lineStrong, width: IrisStroke.hairline),
      ),
      child: Column(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(14, 10, 14, 2),
            child: Row(
              children: [
                IrisLabel(
                  widget.paused
                      ? 'Paused'
                      : 'Queued · after this reply',
                  small: true,
                ),
                const Spacer(),
                if (widget.paused) ...[
                  IrisPressable(
                    onTap: widget.onSendNext,
                    semanticLabel: 'Send next',
                    child: Text('Send next', style: type.label.copyWith(color: palette.accent)),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: IrisSpace.md),
                  IrisPressable(
                    onTap: widget.onClear,
                    semanticLabel: 'Clear',
                    child: Text('Clear', style: type.label.copyWith(color: palette.inkMuted)),
                  ),
                ],
              ],
            ),
          ),
          for (final (index, item) in shown.indexed)
            SizedBox(
              height: 44,
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  const SizedBox(width: 14),
                  Container(
                    width: 20,
                    height: 20,
                    alignment: Alignment.center,
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: palette.accentQuiet,
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                    ),
                    child: Text(
                      '${index + 1}',
                      style: type.codeSmall.copyWith(fontSize: 11, color: palette.accent),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: IrisSpace.md),
                  Expanded(
                    child: Text(
                      item.text,
                      maxLines: 1,
                      overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                      style: type.body.copyWith(fontSize: 14, color: palette.ink),
                    ),
                  ),
                  IrisPressable(
                    onTap: () => widget.onRemove(item.id),
                    semanticLabel: 'Remove message ${index + 1}',
                    child: Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12),
                      child: Icon(Icons.close, size: 16, color: palette.inkMuted),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          if (collapse)
            IrisPressable(
              onTap: () => setState(() => _expanded = true),
              semanticLabel: '${items.length - 2} more queued messages',
              child: SizedBox(
                height: 44,
                child: Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14),
                  child: Align(
                    alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
                    child: Text(
                      '+${items.length - 2} more',
                      style: type.codeSmall.copyWith(fontSize: 12, color: palette.accent),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(14, 0, 14, 8),
            child: Text(
              'Queued messages are held on this phone',
              style: type.caption.copyWith(fontSize: 11, color: palette.inkMuted),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

```dart
/// The action while a reply runs and text is typed: an accent button with
/// the glyph of what sending will do (frame A4).
class _ModeButton extends StatelessWidget {
  const _ModeButton({required this.kind, required this.onTap});

  final SendKind kind;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  static String _hint(SendKind kind) => switch (kind) {
    SendKind.queue => 'Send, queues for after the running reply',
    SendKind.steer => 'Send, steers the running reply',
    SendKind.interrupt => 'Send, interrupts the running reply',
    _ => 'Send',
  };

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final palette = context.palette;
    return IrisPressable(
      onTap: () {
        unawaited(IrisHaptics.commit());
        onTap();
      },
      semanticLabel: _hint(kind),
      scale: .92,
      ensureTapTarget: false,
      child: Container(
        width: 44,
        height: 44,
        alignment: Alignment.center,
        child: Container(
          width: 40,
          height: 40,
          alignment: Alignment.center,
          decoration: BoxDecoration(color: palette.accent, shape: BoxShape.circle),
          child: SendGlyph(kind, color: palette.accentInk, size: 22),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

```dart
/// "While Hermes is replying": what sending a message mid-reply does.
///
/// One Hermes setting with three modes, per profile, read from and written
/// to the server (`display.busy_input_mode`).
library;

import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

import '../../app/providers.dart';
import '../../core/result.dart';
import '../../data/chat/chat_models.dart';
import '../../design/components/sheet.dart';
import '../../design/theme/iris_theme.dart';
import '../../design/tokens/metrics.dart';

/// The profile's current mode, or null when the server has no such setting
/// (the row is then hidden) or there is no live link.
final busyModeProvider = FutureProvider.autoDispose<BusyMode?>((ref) async {
  final chat = await ref.watch(activeChatProvider.future);
  if (chat == null) return null;
  return (await chat.busyMode()).valueOrNull;
});

/// Shows the three modes and returns the one picked (the current one counts:
/// the sheet closes either way). The choice is saved by the caller.
Future<BusyMode?> showBusyPicker(
  BuildContext context, {
  required BusyMode current,
  required String profile,
  required String host,
}) => showIrisSheet<BusyMode>(
  context: context,
  builder: (sheetContext) => _BusySheet(current: current, profile: profile, host: host),
);

class _BusySheet extends StatefulWidget {
  const _BusySheet({required this.current, required this.profile, required this.host});

  final BusyMode current;
  final String profile;
  final String host;

  @override
  State<_BusySheet> createState() => _BusySheetState();
}

class _BusySheetState extends State<_BusySheet> {
  late BusyMode _selected = widget.current;

  void _pick(BusyMode mode) {
    setState(() => _selected = mode);
    // The sheet closes a beat after the choice, so the radio is seen to move.
    final wait = MediaQuery.disableAnimationsOf(context)
        ? Duration.zero
        : const Duration(milliseconds: 250);
    unawaited(
      Future<void>.delayed(wait, () {
        if (mounted) Navigator.of(context).pop(mode);
      }),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final palette = context.palette;
    final type = context.type;
    return IrisSheet(
      title: 'While Hermes is replying',
      subtitle: 'What should happen when you send a message mid-reply?',
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          for (final mode in BusyMode.values)
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: IrisSpace.xs),
              child: Semantics(
                button: true,
                selected: mode == _selected,
                label: '${mode.label}. ${mode.blurb}',
                excludeSemantics: true,
                child: GestureDetector(
                  behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
                  onTap: () => _pick(mode),
                  child: Container(
                    constraints: const BoxConstraints(minHeight: 72),
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 12),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: mode == _selected ? palette.accentQuiet : null,
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
                    ),
                    child: Row(
                      children: [
                        _IrisRadio(selected: mode == _selected),
                        const SizedBox(width: IrisSpace.md),
                        Expanded(
                          child: Column(
                            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                            children: [
                              Text(mode.label, style: type.body.copyWith(fontSize: 15.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.w500, color: palette.ink)),
                              const SizedBox(height: 2),
                              Text(mode.blurb, style: type.body.copyWith(fontSize: 13.5, color: palette.inkMuted)),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: IrisSpace.sm),
            child: Text(
              'Applies to the ${widget.profile} profile on ${widget.host}.',
              style: type.codeSmall.copyWith(fontSize: 11.5, color: palette.inkMuted),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// A small iris as the radio: a ring, and a dot when chosen.
class _IrisRadio extends StatelessWidget {
  const _IrisRadio({required this.selected});

  final bool selected;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final palette = context.palette;
    return Container(
      width: 28,
      height: 28,
      alignment: Alignment.center,
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        shape: BoxShape.circle,
        border: Border.all(
          color: selected ? palette.accent : palette.lineStrong,
          width: selected ? 2.5 : 2,
        ),
      ),
      child: selected
          ? Container(
              width: 10,
              height: 10,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(color: palette.accent, shape: BoxShape.circle),
            )
          : null,
    );
  }
}
```

```dart
Future<void> _pickBusy(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref, BusyMode current) async {
  final host = Uri.tryParse(ref.read(activeServerProvider)?.baseUrl ?? '')?.host ?? '';
  final picked = await showBusyPicker(
    context,
    current: current,
    profile: _titleCase(ref.read(activeProfileProvider)),
    host: host,
  );
  if (picked == null || picked == current || !context.mounted) return;
  final chat = await ref.read(activeChatProvider.future);
  if (chat == null) return;
  final result = await chat.setBusyMode(picked);
  if (!context.mounted) return;
  if (result case Err(:final failure)) {
    showIrisToast(context, message: 'Could not change that: ${ChatController.describe(failure)}');
    return;
  }
  ref.invalidate(busyModeProvider);
}

String _titleCase(String s) => s.isEmpty ? s : '${s[0].toUpperCase()}${s.substring(1)}';
```
(The test expects the toast to start "Could not change", which this does.)

```dart
/// "FROM QUEUE" with a 1pt accent ring, fading after two seconds (frame A4).
class _FromQueueTag extends StatefulWidget {
  const _FromQueueTag();

  @override
  State<_FromQueueTag> createState() => _FromQueueTagState();
}

class _FromQueueTagState extends State<_FromQueueTag> {
  bool _visible = true;
  Timer? _timer;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _timer = Timer(const Duration(seconds: 2), () {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _visible = false);
    });
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _timer?.cancel();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => AnimatedOpacity(
    opacity: _visible ? 1 : 0,
    duration: context.reduceMotion ? Duration.zero : const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
    child: Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: IrisSpace.xs),
      child: Text(
        'FROM QUEUE',
        style: context.type.codeSmall.copyWith(
          fontSize: 10.5,
          letterSpacing: .8,
          color: context.palette.accent,
        ),
      ),
    ),
  );
}
```

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:iris/core/result.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/chat/chat_models.dart';

import 'package:iris/features/chat/model_sheet.dart';

import '../support/harness.dart';

void main() {
  ModelOptions options({required bool reasoning, required bool fast, bool known = true}) => ModelOptions(
    model: 'opus',
    provider: 'anthropic',
    providers: [
      ModelProvider(
        slug: 'anthropic',
        name: 'Anthropic',
        models: const ['opus'],
        isCurrent: true,
        capabilities: known ? {'opus': ModelCaps(reasoning: reasoning, fast: fast)} : const {},
      ),
    ],
  );

  Future<ValueNotifier<ChatState>> pump(
    WidgetTester tester,
    ModelOptions loaded, {
    ChatState state = const ChatState(phase: ChatPhase.ready, model: 'opus', provider: 'anthropic'),
    void Function(ReasoningEffort)? onEffort,
    void Function(bool)? onFast,
  }) async {
    final notifier = ValueNotifier(state);
    addTearDown(notifier.dispose);
    await tester.pumpWidget(themed(Align(
      alignment: Alignment.bottomCenter,
      child: ModelSheet(
        load: () async => Ok(loaded),
        current: 'opus',
        state: notifier,
        onEffort: onEffort,
        onFast: onFast,
      ),
    )));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    return notifier;
  }

  testWidgets('both controls show when the model supports both', (tester) async {
    await pump(tester, options(reasoning: true, fast: true));
    expect(find.text('REASONING EFFORT'), findsOneWidget);
    for (final label in ['None', 'Low', 'Medium', 'High', 'Max']) {
      expect(find.text(label), findsOneWidget);
    }
    expect(find.text('Fast mode'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('unsupported controls are removed, not disabled', (tester) async {
    await pump(tester, options(reasoning: true, fast: false));
    expect(find.text('REASONING EFFORT'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('Fast mode'), findsNothing);
    await pump(tester, options(reasoning: false, fast: false));
    expect(find.text('REASONING EFFORT'), findsNothing);
    expect(find.text('Fast mode'), findsNothing);
  });

  testWidgets('unknown capabilities fall back to what the session reported', (tester) async {
    await pump(
      tester,
      options(reasoning: false, fast: false, known: false),
      state: const ChatState(phase: ChatPhase.ready, model: 'opus', provider: 'anthropic', effort: ReasoningEffort.medium),
    );
    expect(find.text('REASONING EFFORT'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('Fast mode'), findsNothing);
  });

  testWidgets('tapping an effort reports it, and the selected one is marked', (tester) async {
    ReasoningEffort? picked;
    final notifier = await pump(
      tester,
      options(reasoning: true, fast: true),
      onEffort: (e) => picked = e,
    );
    await tester.tap(find.text('High'));
    expect(picked, ReasoningEffort.high);
    notifier.value = notifier.value.copyWith(effort: ReasoningEffort.high);
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.bySemanticsLabel(RegExp('High.*selected', caseSensitive: false)), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('the fast switch reports its change', (tester) async {
    bool? fast;
    await pump(tester, options(reasoning: true, fast: true), onFast: (v) => fast = v);
    await tester.tap(find.byType(Switch).first.evaluate().isEmpty ? find.bySemanticsLabel('Fast mode') : find.byType(Switch).first);
    expect(fast, isTrue);
  });

  testWidgets('a change made mid-reply says it waits for the next turn', (tester) async {
    final notifier = await pump(
      tester,
      options(reasoning: true, fast: true),
      state: const ChatState(phase: ChatPhase.ready, model: 'opus', provider: 'anthropic', turn: TurnPhase.working, effort: ReasoningEffort.high),
      onEffort: (_) {},
    );
    await tester.tap(find.text('Max'));
    notifier.value = notifier.value.copyWith(effort: ReasoningEffort.max);
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('Applies after this reply.'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('high → max'), findsOneWidget);
    notifier.value = notifier.value.copyWith(turn: TurnPhase.idle);
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('Applies after this reply.'), findsNothing);
  });

  testWidgets('five segments wrap at 200% text without overflow', (tester) async {
    tester.view.physicalSize = const Size(320, 800);
    tester.view.devicePixelRatio = 1;
    addTearDown(tester.view.reset);
    await tester.pumpWidget(themed(MediaQuery(
      data: const MediaQueryData(size: Size(320, 800), textScaler: TextScaler.linear(2), disableAnimations: true),

          onEffort: (_) {},
          onFast: (_) {},
        ),
      ),
    )));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(tester.takeException(), isNull);
  });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run, see them fail.**

- [ ] **Step 3: Controller**

```dart
  /// Sets how hard the model thinks, for this conversation. Shown at once;
  /// a refusal brings the previous value back and says why. False when
  /// Hermes did not take it.
  Future<bool> setEffort(ReasoningEffort effort) async {
    final runtimeId = _runtimeId;
    if (runtimeId == null) return false;
    final before = value.effort;
    _update(value.copyWith(effort: effort));
    final result = await _backend.setReasoning(runtimeId, effort);
    if (_disposed) return false;
    if (result case Err(:final failure)) {
      if (before != null) _update(value.copyWith(effort: before));
      onToast?.call(_changeFailed(failure));
      return false;
    }
    return true;
  }

  /// Turns fast mode on or off for this conversation, as [setEffort] does.
  Future<bool> setFast({required bool fast}) async {
    final runtimeId = _runtimeId;
    if (runtimeId == null) return false;
    final before = value.fast;
    _update(value.copyWith(fast: fast));
    final result = await _backend.setFast(runtimeId, fast: fast);
    if (_disposed) return false;
    if (result case Err(:final failure)) {
      if (before != null) _update(value.copyWith(fast: before));
      onToast?.call(_changeFailed(failure));
      return false;
    }
    return true;
  }

  static String _changeFailed(IrisFailure failure) =>
      (failure.rpcError?.isMethodNotFound ?? false)
      ? "This server can't change that."
      : 'Could not change that: ${describe(failure)}';
```
(`rpcError` extension needs `import '../../data/rpc/rpc_frame.dart';` if not already imported by Task 6.)

- [ ] **Step 4: The sheet**

`ModelSheet` gains `this.state` (`ValueListenable<ChatState>?`), `this.onEffort` (`ValueChanged<ReasoningEffort>?`), `this.onFast` (`ValueChanged<bool>?`). In `_list`, after the model groups, when `widget.state != null` add `_Controls` built inside a `ValueListenableBuilder<ChatState>`:

```dart
  Widget _controls(BuildContext context, ModelOptions options) {
    final notifier = widget.state;
    if (notifier == null) return const SizedBox.shrink();
    return ValueListenableBuilder<ChatState>(
      valueListenable: notifier,
      builder: (context, state, _) {
        final model = widget.current ?? options.model;
        final caps = model == null
            ? null
            : options.capsFor(model, provider: state.provider ?? options.provider);
        // When the gateway does not describe the model, what the session
        // itself reported decides: nothing is shown on a guess.
        final showEffort = caps?.reasoning ?? (state.effort != null);
        final showFast = caps?.fast ?? (state.fast != null);
        if (!showEffort && !showFast) return const SizedBox.shrink();
        final palette = context.palette;
        final type = context.type;
        return Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
          children: [
            const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.md),
            if (showEffort) ...[
              const IrisLabel('Reasoning effort', small: true),
              const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.xs),
              _EffortControl(
                selected: state.effort,
                onChanged: (e) {
                  _before ??= state.effort;
                  widget.onEffort?.call(e);
                },
              ),
            ],
            if (showFast) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.sm),
              Container(
                constraints: const BoxConstraints(minHeight: 60),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    Expanded(
                      child: Column(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                        children: [
                          Text('Fast mode', style: type.body.copyWith(fontSize: 15, fontWeight: FontWeight.w500, color: palette.ink)),
                          Text('Quicker replies, costs more', style: type.body.copyWith(fontSize: 13, color: palette.inkMuted)),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                    IrisSwitch(
                      value: state.fast ?? false,
                      semanticLabel: 'Fast mode',
                      onChanged: (on) => widget.onFast?.call(on),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
            if (state.running && _before != null && _before != state.effort)
              _MidTurnNote(from: _before!, to: state.effort),
          ],
        );
      },
    );
  }
```
with `ReasoningEffort? _before;` on the state class (the effort when the sheet opened mid-turn; reset to null when `!state.running`: set inside the builder `if (!state.running) _before = null;` before use — do this assignment in the builder's first line; it is a plain field write, acceptable here), and:

```dart
/// A change made mid-reply waits for the next turn, and says so.
class _MidTurnNote extends StatelessWidget {
  const _MidTurnNote({required this.from, required this.to});

  final ReasoningEffort from;
  final ReasoningEffort? to;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final palette = context.palette;
    final type = context.type;
    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(top: IrisSpace.sm),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(IrisSpace.md),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: palette.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
        border: Border.all(color: palette.line, width: IrisStroke.hairline),
      ),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          const IrisOrb(state: AgentState.streaming, size: 22),
          const SizedBox(width: IrisSpace.md),
          Expanded(
            child: Text('Applies after this reply.', style: type.body.copyWith(fontSize: 13.5, color: palette.ink)),
          ),
          Text(
            '${from.wire} → ${to?.wire ?? from.wire}',
            style: type.codeSmall.copyWith(fontSize: 11.5, color: palette.inkMuted),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
```
and the segmented control:

```dart
/// None, Low, Medium, High, Max in one track. At large text the segments
/// wrap to two rows (3 + 2) rather than clip.
class _EffortControl extends StatelessWidget {
  const _EffortControl({required this.selected, required this.onChanged});

  final ReasoningEffort? selected;
  final ValueChanged<ReasoningEffort> onChanged;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final palette = context.palette;
    final type = context.type;
    final wide = MediaQuery.textScalerOf(context).scale(1) > 1.4;
    Widget segment(ReasoningEffort effort) {
      final on = effort == selected;
      return Semantics(
        button: true,
        selected: on,
        label: '${effort.label}${on ? ', selected' : ''}',
        excludeSemantics: true,
        child: GestureDetector(
          behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
          onTap: () => onChanged(effort),
          child: AnimatedContainer(
            duration: context.reduceMotion ? Duration.zero : const Duration(milliseconds: 180),
            constraints: const BoxConstraints(minHeight: 42, minWidth: 44),
            alignment: Alignment.center,
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 6),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: on ? palette.surfaceRaised : null,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(11),
              border: on ? Border.all(color: palette.lineStrong, width: IrisStroke.hairline) : null,
            ),
            child: Text(
              effort.label,
              style: type.body.copyWith(fontSize: 13.5, color: on ? palette.ink : palette.inkMuted),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      );
    }

    final segments = [for (final e in ReasoningEffort.values) segment(e)];
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(3),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: palette.surfaceSunken,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
        border: Border.all(color: palette.line, width: IrisStroke.hairline),
      ),
      child: wide
          ? Wrap(spacing: 2, runSpacing: 2, children: segments)
          : Row(children: [for (final s in segments) Expanded(child: s)]),
    );
  }
}
```

```dart
enum PromptOutcome { answered, skipped, expired }
final class PromptRecord extends TranscriptItem { const PromptRecord({required String id, required this.title, required this.outcome, this.detail = ''}); }
// prompt_records.dart (pure)
PromptRecord recordForInput(InputRequest r, PromptOutcome o, DateTime at, {required String id});
PromptRecord recordForLogin(LoginRequest r, PromptOutcome o, DateTime at, {required String id});
String clock(DateTime at);                       // 14:02
// ChatController
DateTime Function() clock = DateTime.now;
Future<void> answerInput(InputRequest request, String answer);       // existing; now records a settled line
Future<void> answerLogin(LoginRequest request, {required String identifier, required String password});
Future<void> skipLogin(LoginRequest request);
```

Frames A7, B5.

Rules: every card is a `NeedsYouCard`; the chat orb is needs-you while one is open (already so: `TurnPhase.waiting`); a settled prompt becomes one `SettledLine` that never holds a typed value; withdrawn (`request.cancel`) becomes an expired line.

```dart
  group('prompts', () {
    const unlock = InputRequest(
      requestId: 'srq-1', sessionId: 'run-1', kind: InputKind.vaultUnlock, displayName: '1Password',
    );

      await controller.open();
      backend.emit(InputAsked(unlock));
      await settle();
      backend.answerResult = const Err(IrisFailure.offline());

    test('a withdrawn prompt leaves an expired line', () async {
      final controller = opened();
      await controller.open();

      await settle();
      backend.emit(const RequestWithdrawn('run-1', 'srq-1'));

      final record = controller.value.items.whereType<PromptRecord>().single;
      expect(record.outcome, PromptOutcome.expired);
      expect(record.detail, 'expired 14:02 · Hermes stopped waiting');
    });
  });
```

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import 'package:iris/features/chat/decision_cards.dart';

void main() {
  InputRequest request(InputKind kind, {String hint = '', String site = '', String command = '', String envVar = '', String prompt = '', String displayName = ''}) =>
      InputRequest(requestId: 'srq', sessionId: 's', kind: kind, hint: hint, site: site, command: command, envVar: envVar, prompt: prompt, displayName: displayName);

  Future<List<String>> pumpInput(WidgetTester tester, InputRequest r) async {
    final answers = <String>[];
    await tester.pumpWidget(themed(SingleChildScrollView(child: InputCard(request: r, onAnswer: (v) async => answers.add(v)))));
    return answers;

  group('unlock', () {
    testWidgets('masked field, Unlock sends it, Skip sends nothing', (tester) async {
      final answers = await pumpInput(tester, request(InputKind.vaultUnlock, displayName: '1Password'));
      expect(find.text('1Password is locked'), findsOneWidget);
      expect(find.text('NEEDS YOU · UNLOCK'), findsOneWidget);
      final field = tester.widget<EditableText>(find.byType(EditableText));
      expect(field.obscureText, isTrue);
      expect(field.autocorrect, isFalse);
      await tester.enterText(find.byType(EditableText), 'hunter2');
      await tester.tap(find.text('Unlock'));
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      expect(answers, ['hunter2']);
    });

    testWidgets('Skip sends an empty value', (tester) async {
      final answers = await pumpInput(tester, request(InputKind.vaultUnlock, displayName: '1Password'));
      await tester.tap(find.text('Skip'));
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      expect(answers, ['']);
    });

    testWidgets('Unlock waits for text', (tester) async {
      await pumpInput(tester, request(InputKind.vaultUnlock, displayName: '1Password'));
      await tester.tap(find.text('Unlock'));
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      expect(find.byType(EditableText), findsOneWidget);

    testWidgets('a six-digit hint gives six cells and sends on the sixth digit', (tester) async {
      final answers = await pumpInput(tester, request(InputKind.vaultCode, site: 'GitHub', hint: '6 digits from your authenticator app'));
      expect(find.text('Code for GitHub'), findsOneWidget);
      expect(find.byKey(const ValueKey('code-cell')), findsNWidgets(6));
      await tester.enterText(find.byType(EditableText), '48153');
      await tester.pump();
      expect(answers, isEmpty);
      await tester.enterText(find.byType(EditableText), '481532');
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      expect(answers, ['481532']);

    testWidgets('only digits are accepted in the cells', (tester) async {
      final answers = await pumpInput(tester, request(InputKind.vaultCode, hint: '6 digits'));
      await tester.enterText(find.byType(EditableText), 'ab12');

      expect(tester.widget<EditableText>(find.byType(EditableText)).controller.text, '12');

    testWidgets('any other hint gives one plain field that sends on Send', (tester) async {
      final answers = await pumpInput(tester, request(InputKind.vaultCode, hint: 'a backup code'));
      expect(find.byKey(const ValueKey('code-cell')), findsNothing);
      await tester.enterText(find.byType(EditableText), 'abcd-1234');

      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      expect(answers, ['abcd-1234']);

  group('sudo and secret', () {
    testWidgets('sudo shows the command in mono and uses Deny and Run', (tester) async {
      final answers = await pumpInput(tester, request(InputKind.sudo, command: 'systemctl restart hermes-worker'));
      expect(find.text('NEEDS YOU · SUDO'), findsOneWidget);

    testWidgets('secret names the variable and says it is stored on the server', (tester) async {

      expect(find.text('NEEDS YOU · SECRET'), findsOneWidget);
      expect(find.text('OPENAI_API_KEY'), findsWidgets);
      expect(find.text('Stored on your server. Iris never shows it again.'), findsOneWidget);
      await tester.enterText(find.byType(EditableText), 'sk-123');
      await tester.tap(find.text('Save'));
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      expect(answers, ['sk-123']);
    });
  });

  group('save login', () {
    testWidgets('username and masked password; Save sends both', (tester) async {
      String? id;
      String? pw;
      await tester.pumpWidget(themed(SingleChildScrollView(
        child: LoginCard(
          request: const LoginRequest(requestId: 'srq', sessionId: 's', origin: 'https://github.com', site: 'GitHub'),
          onSave: (identifier, password) async {

      expect(find.text('https://github.com'), findsOneWidget);
      final fields = find.byType(EditableText);

      await tester.enterText(fields.at(0), 'jamie@hermes.dev');
      await tester.enterText(fields.at(1), 'hunter2');
      await tester.tap(find.text('Save'));
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      expect((id, pw), ('jamie@hermes.dev', 'hunter2'));
    });

    testWidgets('Save waits for both fields, and the password can be revealed', (tester) async {
      await tester.pumpWidget(themed(SingleChildScrollView(
        child: LoginCard(
          request: const LoginRequest(requestId: 'srq', sessionId: 's', origin: 'https://github.com'),
          onSave: (_, _) async {},
          onSkip: () async {},
        ),
      )));
      await tester.enterText(find.byType(EditableText).at(0), 'jamie');
      await tester.tap(find.text('Save'));
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      await tester.tap(find.bySemanticsLabel('Show password'));
      await tester.pump();
      expect(tester.widget<EditableText>(find.byType(EditableText).at(1)).obscureText, isFalse);
    });
  });
}
```

```dart
enum PromptOutcome { answered, skipped, expired }

/// What is left of a prompt once it is over: a line in the transcript. It
/// names what was asked and what happened, and never what was typed.
final class PromptRecord extends TranscriptItem {
  const PromptRecord({
    required String id,
    required this.title,
    required this.outcome,
    this.detail = '',
  }) : super(id);

  final String title;
  final PromptOutcome outcome;
  final String detail;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `prompt_records.dart`**

```dart
/// The one-line records prompts leave behind, built from what was asked and
/// how it ended, never from what was typed.
library;

import '../../data/chat/chat_models.dart';

String clock(DateTime at) =>
    '${at.hour.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}:${at.minute.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';

PromptRecord recordForInput(
  InputRequest request,
  PromptOutcome outcome,
  DateTime at, {
  required String id,
}) {
  final time = clock(at);
  final name = request.displayName.isEmpty ? 'Password manager' : request.displayName;
  final site = request.site;
  if (outcome == PromptOutcome.expired) {
    final subject = switch (request.kind) {
      InputKind.vaultUnlock => '$name request',
      InputKind.vaultCode => site.isEmpty ? 'Code request' : '$site code request',
      InputKind.sudo => 'sudo request',
      InputKind.secret => request.envVar.isEmpty ? 'Secret request' : '${request.envVar} request',
    };
    return PromptRecord(
      id: id,
      title: '$subject withdrawn',
      detail: 'expired $time · Hermes stopped waiting',
      outcome: outcome,
    );
  }
  final answered = outcome == PromptOutcome.answered;
  final (title, detail) = switch (request.kind) {
    InputKind.sudo => (
      answered ? 'Ran with sudo' : 'Denied sudo',
      '${request.command.isEmpty ? 'sudo' : request.command} · $time',
    ),
    InputKind.secret => (
      answered
          ? 'Saved ${request.envVar.isEmpty ? 'the secret' : request.envVar}'
          : 'Skipped ${request.envVar.isEmpty ? 'the secret' : request.envVar}',
      answered ? 'value hidden · $time' : 'skipped · $time',
    ),
    InputKind.vaultUnlock => (
      answered ? 'Unlocked $name' : 'Skipped the $name unlock',
      '${answered ? 'answered' : 'skipped'} · $time',
    ),
    InputKind.vaultCode => (
      answered
          ? 'Sent the code${site.isEmpty ? '' : ' for $site'}'
          : 'Skipped the code${site.isEmpty ? '' : ' for $site'}',
      answered ? 'value hidden · $time' : 'skipped · $time',
    ),
  };
  return PromptRecord(id: id, title: title, detail: detail, outcome: outcome);
}

PromptRecord recordForLogin(
  LoginRequest request,
  PromptOutcome outcome,
  DateTime at, {
  required String id,
}) {
  final time = clock(at);
  final site = request.siteName;
  return switch (outcome) {
    PromptOutcome.answered => PromptRecord(
      id: id,
      title: 'Saved $site login',
      detail: 'value hidden · $time',
      outcome: outcome,
    ),
    PromptOutcome.skipped => PromptRecord(
      id: id,
      title: 'Skipped saving the $site login',
      detail: 'skipped · $time',
      outcome: outcome,
    ),
    PromptOutcome.expired => PromptRecord(
      id: id,
      title: '$site login request withdrawn',
      detail: 'expired $time · Hermes stopped waiting',
      outcome: outcome,
    ),
  };
}
```

```dart

  DateTime Function() clock = DateTime.now;
```
Replace `answerInput`:

```dart
  /// Answers a sudo, secret, unlock or code prompt; an empty [answer] skips
  /// it. The card goes at once and a settled line takes its place, naming
  /// what was asked and never what was typed. The answer goes straight to
  /// the backend and is kept nowhere.
  Future<void> answerInput(InputRequest request, String answer) async {
    _update(value.withoutInput(request));
    final result = await _backend.answerInput(request, answer);
    if (_disposed) return;
    switch (result) {
      case Ok():
        _update(
          value.withPromptRecord(
            records.recordForInput(
              request,
              answer.isEmpty ? PromptOutcome.skipped : PromptOutcome.answered,
              clock(),
              id: 'prompt-${value.items.length}',
            ),
          ),
        );
      case Err(:final failure):
        _update(
          value
              .withInput(request)
              .withError('Could not send that: ${describe(failure)}'),
        );
    }
  }

  /// Saves a login the agent asked to save. Both values go straight into
  /// the reply and are kept nowhere.
  Future<void> answerLogin(
    LoginRequest request, {
    required String identifier,
    required String password,
  }) async {
    _update(value.withoutLogin(request));
    final result = await _backend.answerLogin(
      request,
      identifier: identifier,
      password: password,
    );
    if (_disposed) return;
    _finishLogin(
      request,
      result,
      skipped: identifier.isEmpty && password.isEmpty,
    );
  }

  Future<void> skipLogin(LoginRequest request) =>
      answerLogin(request, identifier: '', password: '');

  void _finishLogin(LoginRequest request, Result<void> result, {required bool skipped}) {
    switch (result) {
      case Ok():
        _update(
          value.withPromptRecord(
            records.recordForLogin(
              request,
              skipped ? PromptOutcome.skipped : PromptOutcome.answered,
              clock(),
              id: 'prompt-${value.items.length}',
            ),
          ),
        );
      case Err(:final failure):
        _update(
          value
              .withLogin(request)
              .withError('Could not send that: ${describe(failure)}'),
        );
    }
  }
```
Add `import 'prompt_records.dart' as records;` to the controller (prefixed, because the controller has a field called `clock`). Then replace `_onEvent` with this final version, which keeps Task 8's queue handling and adds the expired line for a withdrawn prompt:

```dart
  void _onEvent(ChatEvent event) {
    final id = event.sessionId;
    if (id.isEmpty || (id != _runtimeId && id != _storedId)) return;
    final withdrawn = event is RequestWithdrawn ? event : null;
    final inputBefore = withdrawn == null
        ? null
        : value.inputs.where((i) => i.requestId == withdrawn.requestId).firstOrNull;
    final loginBefore = withdrawn == null
        ? null
        : value.logins.where((l) => l.requestId == withdrawn.requestId).firstOrNull;
    final wasRunning = value.running;
    _update(value.apply(event));
    if (inputBefore != null) {
      _update(
        value.withPromptRecord(
          records.recordForInput(
            inputBefore,
            PromptOutcome.expired,
            clock(),
            id: 'prompt-${value.items.length}',
          ),
        ),
      );
    } else if (loginBefore != null) {
      _update(
        value.withPromptRecord(
          records.recordForLogin(
            loginBefore,
            PromptOutcome.expired,
            clock(),
            id: 'prompt-${value.items.length}',
          ),
        ),
      );
    }
    if (_reloadWhenIdle && !value.running) unawaited(_reloadNewest());
    if (wasRunning && !value.running) {
      _turnEnded(failedOrStopped: event is TurnFailed || event is TurnInterrupted);
    }
  }
```

(Skipping a login relies on Task 1's `answerLogin`, which sends `value: ''` when both fields are empty.)

```dart
/// Skip on the left, the main action on the right at twice the width, both
/// 48 high (frame A7).
class NeedsYouActions extends StatelessWidget {
  const NeedsYouActions({
    required this.skipLabel,
    required this.primaryLabel,
    this.onSkip,
    this.onPrimary,
    this.busy = false,
    super.key,
  });

  final String skipLabel;
  final String primaryLabel;
  final VoidCallback? onSkip;

  /// Null disables the main action.
  final VoidCallback? onPrimary;
  final bool busy;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final palette = context.palette;
    final type = context.type;
    final needs = palette.colorFor(AgentState.waiting);
    return Row(
      children: [
        Expanded(
          child: Semantics(
            button: true,

            excludeSemantics: true,

              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(width: IrisSpace.sm),
        Expanded(
          flex: 2,
          child: Semantics(
            button: true,
            enabled: onPrimary != null && !busy,
            label: primaryLabel,
            excludeSemantics: true,

              behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,

                  style: type.body.copyWith(
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                    color: onPrimary == null ? palette.inkMuted : palette.accentInk,

              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: `InputCard` on the shared card**

Rewrite `InputCard`'s build in `decision_cards.dart` (keep its constructor `InputCard({required request, required onAnswer})` where `onAnswer` is `Future<void> Function(String)`, and its `_sending` / `_revealed` state):

```dart
  /// Whether a code prompt wants six one-digit cells: only when Hermes's
  /// hint says six. Other codes (backup codes, longer ones) get one field.
  static bool wantsSixCells(String hint) =>
      RegExp(r'\b(6|six)\b', caseSensitive: false).hasMatch(hint);

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final request = widget.request;
    final palette = context.palette;
    final type = context.type;
    final (eyebrow, title, subtitle, skip, primary) = switch (request.kind) {
      InputKind.sudo => ('Needs you · sudo', 'Run this as root?', null, 'Deny', 'Run'),
      InputKind.secret => (
        'Needs you · secret',
        request.prompt.isEmpty ? 'Add a secret' : request.prompt,
        null,
        'Skip',
        'Save',
      ),
      InputKind.vaultUnlock => (
        'Needs you · unlock',
        request.title,
        'Hermes needs your login to carry on.',
        'Skip',
        'Unlock',
      ),
      InputKind.vaultCode => (
        'Needs you · one-time code',
        request.title,
        request.hint.isEmpty ? null : request.hint,
        'Skip',
        'Send',
      ),
    };
    final cells = request.kind == InputKind.vaultCode && InputCard.wantsSixCells(request.hint);
    return NeedsYouCard(
      eyebrow: eyebrow,
      title: title,
      subtitle: subtitle,
      actions: ValueListenableBuilder<TextEditingValue>(
        valueListenable: _text,
        builder: (context, value, _) => NeedsYouActions(
          skipLabel: skip,
          primaryLabel: primary,
          busy: _sending,
          onSkip: () => unawaited(_send('')),
          onPrimary: value.text.isEmpty ? null : () => unawaited(_send(_text.text)),
        ),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          if (request.kind == InputKind.sudo && request.command.isNotEmpty) ...[
            DecoratedBox(
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: palette.surfaceSunken,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                border: Border.all(color: palette.line, width: IrisStroke.hairline),
              ),
              child: Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(IrisSpace.md),
                child: SingleChildScrollView(
                  scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
                  child: Text.rich(
                    TextSpan(
                      style: type.codeSmall.copyWith(fontSize: 13, color: palette.ink),
                      children: [
                        TextSpan(text: r'$ ', style: TextStyle(color: palette.inkMuted)),
                        TextSpan(text: request.command),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.md),
          ],
          if (request.kind == InputKind.secret && request.envVar.isNotEmpty) ...[
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 6),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(color: palette.surfaceSunken, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
              child: Text(request.envVar, style: type.codeSmall.copyWith(fontSize: 13, color: palette.ink)),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.md),
          ],
          if (cells)
            _CodeCells(controller: _text, onFull: (code) => unawaited(_send(code)))
          else
            IrisField(
              controller: _text,
              hint: switch (request.kind) {
                InputKind.sudo => 'Password',
                InputKind.secret => 'Value',
                InputKind.vaultUnlock => 'Master password',
                InputKind.vaultCode => 'Code',
              },
              mono: true,
              obscure: request.kind != InputKind.vaultCode && !_revealed,
              keyboardType: request.kind == InputKind.vaultCode ? TextInputType.visiblePassword : null,
              autocorrect: false,
              enableSuggestions: false,
              autofocus: true,
              onSubmitted: (value) => unawaited(_send(value)),
              trailing: request.kind == InputKind.vaultCode
                  ? null
                  : IrisPressable(
                      onTap: () => setState(() => _revealed = !_revealed),
                      semanticLabel: _revealed ? 'Hide password' : 'Show password',
                      child: Icon(
                        _revealed ? Icons.visibility_off_outlined : Icons.visibility_outlined,
                        size: IrisSize.icon,
                        color: palette.inkMuted,
                      ),
                    ),
            ),
          if (request.kind == InputKind.secret) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.sm),
            Text(
              'Stored on your server. Iris never shows it again.',
              style: type.codeSmall.copyWith(fontSize: 12, color: palette.inkMuted),
            ),
          ],
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
```
and the cells:

```dart
/// Six one-digit cells over one hidden numeric field. The sixth digit sends.
class _CodeCells extends StatefulWidget {
  const _CodeCells({required this.controller, required this.onFull});

  final TextEditingController controller;
  final ValueChanged<String> onFull;

  @override
  State<_CodeCells> createState() => _CodeCellsState();
}

class _CodeCellsState extends State<_CodeCells> {
  final _focus = FocusNode();

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _focus.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final palette = context.palette;
    final type = context.type;
    final needs = palette.colorFor(AgentState.waiting);
    return GestureDetector(
      behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
      onTap: _focus.requestFocus,
      child: Stack(
        children: [
          // The real field: numeric, six long, invisible.
          Opacity(
            opacity: 0,
            child: SizedBox(
              height: 58,
              child: TextField(
                controller: widget.controller,
                focusNode: _focus,
                autofocus: true,
                keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
                autocorrect: false,
                enableSuggestions: false,
                inputFormatters: [FilteringTextInputFormatter.digitsOnly, LengthLimitingTextInputFormatter(6)],
                onChanged: (text) {
                  setState(() {});
                  if (text.length == 6) widget.onFull(text);
                },
              ),
            ),
          ),
          IgnorePointer(
            child: ValueListenableBuilder<TextEditingValue>(
              valueListenable: widget.controller,
              builder: (context, value, _) => Row(
                children: [
                  for (var i = 0; i < 6; i++) ...[
                    if (i > 0) const SizedBox(width: IrisSpace.xs),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Container(
                        key: const ValueKey('code-cell'),
                        height: 58,
                        alignment: Alignment.center,
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: palette.surfaceSunken,
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                          border: Border.all(
                            color: i == value.text.length ? needs : palette.lineStrong,
                            width: i == value.text.length ? 1.5 : IrisStroke.hairline,
                          ),
                        ),
                        child: Text(
                          i < value.text.length ? value.text[i] : '',
                          style: type.codeSmall.copyWith(fontSize: 26, color: palette.ink),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
```
(imports: `package:flutter/services.dart`, `needs_you_card.dart`, `palette.dart`.) In the widget tests, `find.byType(EditableText)` finds the hidden field (it is still built); `tester.enterText` into it triggers `onChanged`.
