```dart
/// A prompt to save a login: username, password, Save or Skip (frame A7).
/// Both values go straight to [onSave] and are held nowhere else.
library;

import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../data/chat/chat_models.dart';
import '../../design/components/controls.dart';
import '../../design/components/needs_you_card.dart';
import '../../design/components/primitives.dart';
import '../../design/theme/iris_theme.dart';
import '../../design/tokens/metrics.dart';

class LoginCard extends StatefulWidget {
  const LoginCard({
    required this.request,
    required this.onSave,
    required this.onSkip,
    super.key,
  });

  final LoginRequest request;
  final Future<void> Function(String identifier, String password) onSave;
  final Future<void> Function() onSkip;

  @override
  State<LoginCard> createState() => _LoginCardState();
}

class _LoginCardState extends State<LoginCard> {
  final _identifier = TextEditingController();
  final _password = TextEditingController();
  bool _revealed = false;
  bool _sending = false;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _identifier.dispose();
    _password.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _save() async {
    setState(() => _sending = true);
    await widget.onSave(_identifier.text, _password.text);
    if (mounted) setState(() => _sending = false);
  }

  Future<void> _skip() async {
    setState(() => _sending = true);
    await widget.onSkip();
    if (mounted) setState(() => _sending = false);
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final palette = context.palette;
    final type = context.type;
    final request = widget.request;
    final listenable = Listenable.merge([_identifier, _password]);
    return NeedsYouCard(
      eyebrow: 'Needs you · save login',
      title: 'Save to your password manager?',
      actions: ListenableBuilder(
        listenable: listenable,
        builder: (context, _) => NeedsYouActions(
          skipLabel: 'Skip',
          primaryLabel: 'Save',
          busy: _sending,
          onSkip: () => unawaited(_skip()),
          onPrimary: _identifier.text.isEmpty || _password.text.isEmpty
              ? null
              : () => unawaited(_save()),
        ),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              Container(
                width: 36,
                height: 36,
                alignment: Alignment.center,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(color: palette.surfaceSunken, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(11)),
                child: Text(
                  request.siteName.isEmpty ? '?' : request.siteName[0].toUpperCase(),
                  style: type.body.copyWith(fontWeight: FontWeight.w500, color: palette.ink),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: IrisSpace.md),
              Expanded(
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Text(request.siteName, style: type.body.copyWith(fontWeight: FontWeight.w500, color: palette.ink)),
                    Text(request.origin, style: type.codeSmall.copyWith(fontSize: 11.5, color: palette.inkMuted)),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.md),
          const IrisLabel('Username', small: true),
          const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.xs),
          IrisField(
            controller: _identifier,
            hint: 'Username or email',
            keyboardType: TextInputType.emailAddress,
            autocorrect: false,
            enableSuggestions: false,
            autofocus: true,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.md),
          const IrisLabel('Password', small: true),
          const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.xs),
          IrisField(
            controller: _password,
            hint: 'Password',
            mono: true,
            obscure: !_revealed,
            autocorrect: false,
            enableSuggestions: false,
            trailing: IrisPressable(
              onTap: () => setState(() => _revealed = !_revealed),
              semanticLabel: _revealed ? 'Hide password' : 'Show password',
              child: Icon(
                _revealed ? Icons.visibility_off_outlined : Icons.visibility_outlined,
                size: IrisSize.icon,
                color: palette.inkMuted,
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

### Task 11: The connection card

**Model: opus.** Deadlines, updates that race responses, and credential handling.

**Files:**
- Create: `lib/features/chat/connection_card.dart`
- Modify: `lib/data/chat/chat_backend.dart` (resume's `pending_connection`), `lib/features/chat/chat_controller.dart`, `lib/features/chat/chat_screen.dart`
- Test: `test/features/connection_card_test.dart`; extend `test/data/chat_backend_test.dart`, `test/features/chat_controller_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ConnectionRequest`/`ConnectionTarget`/`TargetControl`/`TargetAnswer` (Task 1), `ChatBackend.answerConnection`, `NeedsYouCard` tints, `openLink` (`lib/features/chat/links.dart`), `IrisField`, `IrisChip.state`, `PromptRecord` (Task 10).
- Produces:

```dart
// ChatController
Future<void> answerConnectionTarget(ConnectionRequest request, TargetAnswer answer);
Future<void> settleConnection(ConnectionRequest request);   // Continue
Future<void> openSignIn(ConnectionTarget target);
void expireConnection(String opId);
// connection_card.dart
class ConnectionCard extends StatefulWidget {
  const ConnectionCard({required this.request, required this.onAnswer, required this.onContinue, required this.onOpenSignIn, required this.onExpired, this.now = DateTime.now});
}
```

Frame A8.

Behaviour (spec): one row per target; each shows a state chip and only the controls it needs now (`TargetControl`). Sign-in opens the browser; the row then shows "Waiting for sign-in…" with Reopen and follows `connection.update`; no polling. MCP credentials: one field per `required_env` (masked when `secret`), then Approve. Anything the phone can't do: "Finish this on Hermes Desktop" and Skip. Continue sends `settled_by: continue` and counts what is open as skipped; it is a ghost button while anything is pending and filled once everything is settled. The card closes when Hermes reports `settled`; when the deadline passes it becomes an expired line.

```dart
  group('connection card', () {
    ConnectionRequest card() => ConnectionRequest.parse(
      Json.of({
        'op_id': 'op1',
        'timeout_seconds': 300,
        'targets': [
          {'name': 'Linear', 'kind': 'connector', 'action': 'authorize', 'state': 'pending', 'connect_url': 'https://linear.app/oauth'},
          {'name': 'GitHub', 'kind': 'mcp', 'action': 'connect', 'state': 'pending'},
        ],
      }),
      sessionId: 'run-1',
    )!;

    Future<ChatController> withCard() async {
      final controller = controllerFor()..clock = () => DateTime(2026, 10, 16, 14, 7);
      await controller.open();
      backend.emit(ConnectionAsked(card()));
      await settle();
      return controller;
    }

    test('approving one target sends just that target and keeps the card', () async {
      final controller = await withCard();
      await controller.answerConnectionTarget(card(), const TargetAnswer.approved('GitHub', env: {'GITHUB_TOKEN': 'ghp_x'}));
      final (op, answers, settle) = backend.connectionAnswers.single;
      expect(op, 'op1');
      expect(answers.single.name, 'GitHub');
      expect(settle, isFalse);
      expect(controller.value.connections, hasLength(1));
    });

    test('credentials are never kept in state', () async {
      final controller = await withCard();
      await controller.answerConnectionTarget(card(), const TargetAnswer.approved('GitHub', env: {'GITHUB_TOKEN': 'ghp_secret'}));
      expect('${controller.value.connections}${controller.value.items}', isNot(contains('ghp_secret')));
    });

    test('a failed answer says so and the card stays', () async {
      final toasts = <String>[];
      final controller = await withCard()
        ..onToast = (message, {actionLabel, onAction}) => toasts.add(message);
      backend.connectionResult = const Err(IrisFailure.offline());
      await controller.answerConnectionTarget(card(), const TargetAnswer.skipped('Linear'));
      expect(toasts.single, startsWith('Could not send'));
      expect(controller.value.connections, hasLength(1));
    });

    test('Continue skips everything still open and closes the card', () async {
      final controller = await withCard();
      await controller.settleConnection(card());
      final (_, answers, settle) = backend.connectionAnswers.single;
      expect(settle, isTrue);
      expect(answers.map((a) => (a.name, a.approved)), [('Linear', false), ('GitHub', false)]);
      expect(controller.value.connections, isEmpty);
    });

    test('Continue leaves resolved targets alone', () async {
      final controller = await withCard();
      backend.emit(ConnectionChanged('run-1', opId: 'op1', targets: [card().targets.first.withState(TargetState.connected)], settled: false));
      await settle();
      await controller.settleConnection(controller.value.connections.single);
      expect(backend.connectionAnswers.single.$2.map((a) => a.name), ['GitHub']);
    });

    test('an expired card becomes an expired line', () async {
      final controller = await withCard();
      controller.expireConnection('op1');
      expect(controller.value.connections, isEmpty);
      final record = controller.value.items.whereType<PromptRecord>().single;
      expect(record.outcome, PromptOutcome.expired);
      expect(record.detail, 'expired 14:07 · Hermes stopped waiting');
    });

    test('expiring a card that is not open does nothing', () async {
      final controller = await withCard();
      controller.expireConnection('nope');
      expect(controller.value.connections, hasLength(1));
    });
  });
```
(Imports: `package:iris/core/json.dart`, `package:iris/data/chat/connection_models.dart`.)

void main() {

              'required_env': [
                {'name': 'GITHUB_TOKEN', 'required': true, 'secret': true, 'default': '', 'prompt': 'Personal access token with repo scope.'},
              ],
            },
            {'name': 'release-notes', 'kind': 'skill', 'action': 'enable', 'state': 'pending'},

  )!;

  }) => tester.pumpWidget(themed(SingleChildScrollView(
    child: ConnectionCard(
      request: r,
      now: () => now,

  testWidgets('after returning from sign-in the row waits for Hermes and offers Reopen', (tester) async {

    TargetAnswer? answered;
    await pump(tester, request(), onAnswer: (a) => answered = a);
    expect(find.text('GITHUB_TOKEN'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('Personal access token with repo scope.'), findsOneWidget);
    final field = find.descendant(of: find.byKey(const ValueKey('target-GitHub')), matching: find.byType(EditableText));
    expect(tester.widget<EditableText>(field).obscureText, isTrue);
    await tester.enterText(field, 'ghp_x');
    await tester.tap(find.descendant(of: find.byKey(const ValueKey('target-GitHub')), matching: find.text('Approve')));
    expect(answered?.approved, isTrue);
    expect(answered?.env, {'GITHUB_TOKEN': 'ghp_x'});
  });

  testWidgets('Approve waits for the required values', (tester) async {
    TargetAnswer? answered;

    await tester.tap(find.descendant(of: find.byKey(const ValueKey('target-GitHub')), matching: find.text('Approve')));
    expect(answered, isNull);

  testWidgets('an error from Hermes shows under the field', (tester) async {

  testWidgets('enable is one tap, skip is one tap', (tester) async {

  testWidgets('desktop-only rows say so and can be skipped', (tester) async {

  testWidgets('Continue is ghost while anything is pending and filled once settled', (tester) async {
    var continued = 0;
    await pump(tester, request(), onContinue: () => continued++);
    await tester.tap(find.text('Continue'));

    expect(find.byKey(const ValueKey('continue-ghost')), findsOneWidget);

    await pump(tester, request(targets: [

  });

  testWidgets('an unrecognised target falls back to desktop-only', (tester) async {
    await pump(tester, request(targets: [
      {'name': 'Mystery', 'kind': 'teleporter', 'action': 'beam', 'state': 'glowing'},
    ]));
    expect(find.text('Finish this on Hermes Desktop'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(tester.takeException(), isNull);
  });

  testWidgets('a deadline already past reports expiry once', (tester) async {
    var expired = 0;
    await pump(tester, request(seconds: 0), onExpired: () => expired++);
    await tester.pump();
    expect(expired, 1);
  });

  testWidgets('the countdown ticks and reports expiry when it reaches zero', (tester) async {
    var clockNow = now;
    var expired = 0;
    await tester.pumpWidget(themed(SingleChildScrollView(
      child: ConnectionCard(
        request: request(seconds: 2),
        now: () => clockNow,
        onAnswer: (_) {},
        onContinue: () {},
        onOpenSignIn: (_) {},
        onExpired: () => expired++,
      ),
    )));
    expect(find.text('00:02'), findsOneWidget);
    clockNow = now.add(const Duration(seconds: 3));
    await tester.pump(const Duration(seconds: 1));
    expect(expired, 1);
  });

  testWidgets('no overflow at 320pt and 200% text', (tester) async {
    tester.view.physicalSize = const Size(320, 900);
    tester.view.devicePixelRatio = 1;
    addTearDown(tester.view.reset);

          onAnswer: (_) {},
          onContinue: () {},
          onOpenSignIn: (_) {},
          onExpired: () {},
        ),
      ),
    )));
    expect(tester.takeException(), isNull);
  });
}

- [ ] **Step 4: Controller**

```dart
  /// Approves or skips one target of an open card. The card stays: Hermes
  /// reports the new state in a `connection.update`. Credentials in
  /// [answer] go straight to the wire and are kept nowhere.
  Future<void> answerConnectionTarget(
    ConnectionRequest request,
    TargetAnswer answer,
  ) async {
    final result = await _backend.answerConnection(request, [answer]);
    if (_disposed) return;
    if (result case Err(:final failure)) {
      onToast?.call('Could not send that: ${describe(failure)}');
    }
  }

  /// Continue: settles the card, counting every target still open as
  /// skipped. The card closes when Hermes says so; if it does not, it
  /// closes here once the answer was accepted.
  Future<void> settleConnection(ConnectionRequest request) async {
    final open = [
      for (final target in request.targets)
        if (!target.state.resolved) TargetAnswer.skipped(target.name),
    ];
    final result = await _backend.answerConnection(request, open, settle: true);
    if (_disposed) return;
    switch (result) {
      case Ok():
        _update(value.withoutConnection(request.opId));
      case Err(:final failure):
        onToast?.call('Could not send that: ${describe(failure)}');
    }
  }

  /// Opens a target's sign-in page in the browser. Hermes then reports
  /// progress on its own.
  Future<void> openSignIn(ConnectionTarget target) async {
    final url = target.connectUrl;
    if (url == null) return;
    await openLink(url);
  }

  /// The card's deadline passed. It leaves an expired line, as a withdrawn
  /// prompt does.
  void expireConnection(String opId) {
    if (!value.connections.any((c) => c.opId == opId)) return;
    final at = clock();
    _update(
      value
          .withoutConnection(opId)
          .withPromptRecord(
            PromptRecord(
              id: 'prompt-${value.items.length}',
              title: 'Connection setup',
              detail: 'expired ${records.clock(at)} · Hermes stopped waiting',
              outcome: PromptOutcome.expired,
            ),
          ),
    );
  }
```
(The `records` prefix is the import added in Task 10. `openLink` is from `links.dart`: add `import 'links.dart';`.)

- [ ] **Step 5: `connection_card.dart`**

```dart
/// What the agent wants to connect, one row per target (frame A8).
///
/// Each row shows a state chip and only the controls it needs right now.
/// The countdown to Hermes's deadline sits in the header in mono. The card
/// does only what a phone can: sign in through the browser, type the
/// credentials an MCP server needs, enable a skill. Everything else says to
/// finish on Hermes Desktop and can be skipped.
library;

import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../data/chat/connection_models.dart';
import '../../design/components/controls.dart';
import '../../design/components/iris_orb.dart';
import '../../design/components/needs_you_card.dart';
import '../../design/components/primitives.dart';
import '../../design/theme/iris_theme.dart';
import '../../design/tokens/metrics.dart';
import '../../design/tokens/palette.dart';

class ConnectionCard extends StatefulWidget {
  const ConnectionCard({
    required this.request,
    required this.onAnswer,
    required this.onContinue,
    required this.onOpenSignIn,
    required this.onExpired,
    this.now = DateTime.now,
    super.key,
  });

  final ConnectionRequest request;
  final ValueChanged<TargetAnswer> onAnswer;
  final VoidCallback onContinue;
  final ValueChanged<ConnectionTarget> onOpenSignIn;

  /// Called once, when the deadline passes.
  final VoidCallback onExpired;

  /// The wall clock, so tests can move time.
  final DateTime Function() now;

  @override
  State<ConnectionCard> createState() => _ConnectionCardState();
}

class _ConnectionCardState extends State<ConnectionCard> {
  Timer? _tick;
  bool _expiredReported = false;
  final Set<String> _waiting = {};
  final Map<String, Map<String, TextEditingController>> _env = {};

  Duration get _left => widget.request.deadline.difference(widget.now());

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _tick = Timer.periodic(const Duration(seconds: 1), (_) => _check());
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) => _check());
  }

  void _check() {
    if (!mounted) return;
    if (_left <= Duration.zero && !_expiredReported) {
      _expiredReported = true;
      widget.onExpired();
    }
    setState(() {});
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _tick?.cancel();
    for (final fields in _env.values) {
      for (final controller in fields.values) {
        controller.dispose();
      }
    }
    super.dispose();
  }

  String get _countdown {
    final left = _left.isNegative ? Duration.zero : _left;
    String two(int n) => n.toString().padLeft(2, '0');
    return '${two(left.inMinutes)}:${two(left.inSeconds % 60)}';
  }

  TextEditingController _field(String target, String name) =>
      (_env[target] ??= {})[name] ??= TextEditingController();

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final request = widget.request;
    final palette = context.palette;
    final type = context.type;
    final needs = palette.colorFor(AgentState.waiting);
    final settled = request.targets.every((t) => t.state.resolved);
    final borderColor = settled ? palette.colorFor(AgentState.done) : needs;
    return DecoratedBox(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: palette.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
        border: Border.all(
          color: borderColor.withValues(alpha: settled ? .4 : .45),
          width: IrisStroke.hairline,
        ),
      ),
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            Row(
              children: [
                IrisLabel('Connect · ${request.targets.length} items', color: needs, small: true),
                const Spacer(),
                Text(
                  _countdown,
                  style: type.codeSmall.copyWith(fontSize: 11, color: needs),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.xs),
            for (final (index, target) in request.targets.indexed) ...[
              if (index > 0) const IrisDivider(),
              _TargetRow(
                key: ValueKey('target-${target.name}'),
                target: target,
                waiting: _waiting.contains(target.name),
                field: (name) => _field(target.name, name),
                onSignIn: () {
                  setState(() => _waiting.add(target.name));
                  widget.onOpenSignIn(target);
                },
                onAnswer: widget.onAnswer,
              ),
            ],
            const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.sm),
            Semantics(
              button: true,
              label: 'Continue',
              excludeSemantics: true,
              child: GestureDetector(
                behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
                onTap: widget.onContinue,
                child: Container(
                  key: ValueKey(settled ? 'continue-filled' : 'continue-ghost'),
                  height: 48,
                  alignment: Alignment.center,
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: settled ? palette.accent : null,
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                    border: settled ? null : Border.all(color: palette.lineStrong, width: IrisStroke.hairline),
                  ),
                  child: Text(
                    'Continue',
                    style: type.body.copyWith(
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                      color: settled ? palette.accentInk : palette.inkMuted,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _TargetRow extends StatelessWidget {
  const _TargetRow({
    required this.target,
    required this.waiting,
    required this.field,
    required this.onSignIn,
    required this.onAnswer,
    super.key,
  });

  final ConnectionTarget target;
  final bool waiting;
  final TextEditingController Function(String name) field;
  final VoidCallback onSignIn;
  final ValueChanged<TargetAnswer> onAnswer;

  static String _kind(TargetKind kind) => switch (kind) {
    TargetKind.connector => 'connector',
    TargetKind.mcp => 'MCP server',
    TargetKind.plugin => 'plugin',
    TargetKind.skill => 'skill',
    TargetKind.other => 'item',
  };

  static String _action(TargetAction action) => switch (action) {
    TargetAction.authorize => 'authorise',
    TargetAction.connect => 'connect',
    TargetAction.enable => 'enable',
    TargetAction.install => 'install',
    TargetAction.reconnect => 'reconnect',
    TargetAction.other => 'set up',
  };

  (String, AgentState?) _chip() => switch (target.state) {
    TargetState.pending => ('Pending', AgentState.waiting),
    TargetState.initiated => ('Opened', AgentState.loading),
    TargetState.connected => ('Connected', AgentState.done),
    TargetState.failed => ('Failed', AgentState.failed),
    TargetState.skipped => ('Skipped', AgentState.rest),
    TargetState.expired => ('Expired', AgentState.offline),
    TargetState.notConnected => ('Not connected', AgentState.rest),
    TargetState.other => ('Pending', AgentState.waiting),
  };

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final palette = context.palette;
    final type = context.type;
    final (label, state) = _chip();
    final inWaiting = waiting && target.state == TargetState.pending;
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 10),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              Container(
                width: 36,
                height: 36,
                alignment: Alignment.center,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(color: palette.surfaceSunken, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(11)),
                child: Icon(
                  switch (target.kind) {
                    TargetKind.connector => Icons.link,
                    TargetKind.mcp => Icons.dns_outlined,
                    TargetKind.plugin => Icons.extension_outlined,
                    TargetKind.skill => Icons.auto_awesome_outlined,
                    TargetKind.other => Icons.help_outline,
                  },
                  size: 18,
                  color: palette.ink,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: IrisSpace.md),
              Expanded(
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Text(target.name, style: type.body.copyWith(fontSize: 14.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.w500, color: palette.ink)),
                    Text(
                      '${_kind(target.kind)} · ${_action(target.action)}',
                      style: type.codeSmall.copyWith(fontSize: 11, color: palette.inkMuted),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              IrisChip.state(label, state ?? AgentState.rest, palette),
            ],
          ),
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 48),
            child: _controls(context),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _controls(BuildContext context) {
    final palette = context.palette;
    final type = context.type;
    final control = target.control;
    Widget skip() => IrisPressable(
      onTap: () => onAnswer(TargetAnswer.skipped(target.name)),
      semanticLabel: 'Skip ${target.name}',
      child: Text('Skip', style: type.label.copyWith(color: palette.accent)),
    );
    switch (control) {
      case TargetControl.none:
        return target.detail.isEmpty
            ? const SizedBox.shrink()
            : Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: IrisSpace.xs),
                child: Text(target.detail, style: type.codeSmall.copyWith(fontSize: 11.5, color: palette.colorFor(AgentState.failed))),
              );
      case TargetControl.signIn:
        if (waiting && target.state != TargetState.connected) {
          return Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: IrisSpace.sm),
            child: Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(IrisSpace.md),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(color: palette.surfaceSunken, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  const IrisOrb(state: AgentState.loading, size: 22),
                  const SizedBox(width: IrisSpace.md),
                  Expanded(child: Text('Waiting for sign-in…', style: type.body.copyWith(fontSize: 13.5, color: palette.ink))),
                  IrisPressable(
                    onTap: onSignIn,
                    semanticLabel: 'Reopen sign-in',
                    child: Text('Reopen', style: type.label.copyWith(color: palette.accent)),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          );
        }
        return Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: IrisSpace.sm),
          child: Row(
            children: [
              IrisButton(label: 'Open sign-in', tone: IrisButtonTone.ghost, onPressed: onSignIn),
              const SizedBox(width: IrisSpace.sm),
              Icon(Icons.open_in_new, size: 14, color: palette.inkMuted),
              const Spacer(),
              skip(),
            ],
          ),
        );
      case TargetControl.credentials:
        return _Credentials(target: target, field: field, onAnswer: onAnswer);
      case TargetControl.approve:
        return Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: IrisSpace.sm),
          child: Row(
            children: [
              IrisButton(
                label: target.action == TargetAction.enable ? 'Enable' : 'Approve',
                tone: IrisButtonTone.primary,
                onPressed: () => onAnswer(TargetAnswer.approved(target.name)),
              ),
              const Spacer(),
              skip(),
            ],
          ),
        );
      case TargetControl.desktopOnly:
        return Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: IrisSpace.sm),
          child: Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: IrisSpace.md, vertical: IrisSpace.sm),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(color: palette.surfaceSunken, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
            child: Row(
              children: [
                Icon(Icons.desktop_windows_outlined, size: 16, color: palette.inkMuted),
                const SizedBox(width: IrisSpace.sm),
                Expanded(
                  child: Text('Finish this on Hermes Desktop', style: type.body.copyWith(fontSize: 13, color: palette.inkMuted)),
                ),
                skip(),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        );
    }
  }
}

/// The values an MCP server needs, then Approve. Values live in controllers
/// that are disposed with the card and go straight into the answer.
class _Credentials extends StatelessWidget {
  const _Credentials({required this.target, required this.field, required this.onAnswer});

  final ConnectionTarget target;
  final TextEditingController Function(String name) field;
  final ValueChanged<TargetAnswer> onAnswer;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final palette = context.palette;
    final type = context.type;
    final controllers = [for (final f in target.requiredEnv) field(f.name)];
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
      children: [
        for (final (i, env) in target.requiredEnv.indexed) ...[
          const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.sm),
          IrisLabel(env.name, small: true),
          const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.xs),
          IrisField(
            controller: controllers[i],
            hint: env.fallback.isEmpty ? 'Value' : env.fallback,
            mono: true,
            obscure: env.secret,
            autocorrect: false,
            enableSuggestions: false,
          ),
          if (env.prompt.isNotEmpty) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: 4),
            Text(env.prompt, style: type.body.copyWith(fontSize: 12, color: palette.inkMuted)),
          ],
        ],
        if (target.detail.isNotEmpty) ...[
          const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.xs),
          Text(target.detail, style: type.codeSmall.copyWith(fontSize: 11.5, color: palette.colorFor(AgentState.failed))),
        ],
        const SizedBox(height: IrisSpace.sm),
        ListenableBuilder(
          listenable: Listenable.merge(controllers),
          builder: (context, _) {
            final ready = [
              for (final (i, env) in target.requiredEnv.indexed)
                if (env.required) controllers[i].text.isNotEmpty,
            ].every((ok) => ok);
            return Row(
              children: [
                IrisButton(
                  label: 'Approve',
                  tone: IrisButtonTone.primary,
                  onPressed: ready
                      ? () => onAnswer(
                          TargetAnswer.approved(
                            target.name,
                            env: {
                              for (final (i, env) in target.requiredEnv.indexed)
                                if (controllers[i].text.isNotEmpty) env.name: controllers[i].text,
                            },
                          ),
                        )
                      : null,
                ),
                const Spacer(),
                IrisPressable(
                  onTap: () => onAnswer(TargetAnswer.skipped(target.name)),
                  semanticLabel: 'Skip ${target.name}',
                  child: Text('Skip', style: type.label.copyWith(color: palette.accent)),
                ),
              ],
            );
          },
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}
```
The test finds the countdown as `04:32` for 272 seconds, `Pending` four times, `Failed` once. `IrisButton.onPressed: null` disables it (the tests tap it and expect no answer). Note `IrisChip.state(label, state, palette)` has the signature used by the home bar. `NeedsYouCard` is not used here (this card has its own header); the import is for tints only: remove it if unused.

- [ ] **Step 6: The screen**

In `_decision`, before the `logins` branch (a connection card is the most specific prompt):

```dart
    if (state.connections.isNotEmpty) {
      final request = state.connections.first;
      return _Decision(
        key: ValueKey('connection-${request.opId}'),
        child: ConnectionCard(
          request: request,
          onAnswer: (answer) => unawaited(_chat.answerConnectionTarget(request, answer)),
          onContinue: () => unawaited(_chat.settleConnection(request)),
          onOpenSignIn: (target) => unawaited(_chat.openSignIn(target)),
          onExpired: () => _chat.expireConnection(request.opId),
        ),
      );
    }
```
(import `connection_card.dart`.) The decision area is not scrollable; a card with many rows must scroll. Wrap the decision slot in `ConstrainedBox(constraints: BoxConstraints(maxHeight: MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).height * .6), child: SingleChildScrollView(child: …))` for the connection card only (inside `_Decision`'s child), so a long list never pushes the composer off screen. Add a screen test: a card with eight targets at 320×568 renders without overflow and the composer is still visible.

### Task 12: Restyle the unmocked features to the design (B2, B3, B6, B7, B8, B9, B10)

B1 (steering) and B5 (sudo and secret) were done in Tasks 8 and 10; B4 (search) is Task 13. This task is six sub-steps, **each with its own commit**. For each one, open the named frame in `design/Iris Phase 2.dc.html`, build to its Notes block, and prove it with the tests listed. Where a number appears below it is copied from that Notes block.

**Files (by sub-step):** see each.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `NeedsYouCard`/`SendGlyph` tints (Task 3), `ChatController.branch(summarise: true)` and `_branch` on the screen (Task 7), `ChatState.queue` footer layout (Task 8).
- Produces:

```dart
// chat_state.dart
int ChatState.attempt                       // 1, increments on retry, resets on a new user turn
// transcript_view.dart
TranscriptEntry({…, int attempt = 1, VoidCallback? onStop})
// controls.dart
IrisRing({…, Color? color})                 // overrides gaugeTint
// toast.dart
showIrisToast({…, Duration? visibleFor})
// attachment_picker.dart
enum AttachSource { photos, files }
typedef PickFiles = Future<List<Attachment>> Function(AttachSource source);
// voice_recorder.dart
abstract interface class VoiceRecorder { …, Stream<double> get level; }   // 0..1, about 10 a second
```

#### 12a — The failed turn (frame B2)

**Files:** `lib/features/chat/transcript_view.dart` (`_FailedCard`), `lib/features/chat/chat_state.dart`, `lib/features/chat/chat_controller.dart`, `lib/features/chat/chat_screen.dart`; test `test/features/failed_card_test.dart`.

Design: radius 18, pad 14, failed 8% fill with a 1pt failed 32% line; the failed orb (28) shakes once; title "The turn stopped" 15/500 ink; the plain cause 13.5 muted; **Retry** 44 high in the failed fill, **Details** ghost with a `lineStrong` edge, widths 2:1. Retrying: the card goes neutral (surface, `lineStrong`), a connecting orb, mono "attempt n", a ghost **Stop**.

- [ ] **Step 1: Failing test**

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/chat/chat_models.dart';
import 'package:iris/features/chat/transcript_view.dart';

import '../support/harness.dart';

void main() {
  const notice = Notice(
    id: 'n',
    text: 'Hermes lost the gateway while reading logs. 502 after 30s on read_logs',
    isError: true,
    retryable: true,
  );

  Widget card({bool retrying = false, int attempt = 1, VoidCallback? onRetry, VoidCallback? onStop}) => themed(
    SingleChildScrollView(
      child: TranscriptEntry(
        notice,
        onRetry: onRetry ?? () {},
        retrying: retrying,
        attempt: attempt,
        onStop: onStop,
      ),
    ),
  );

  testWidgets('says the turn stopped and offers Retry and Details', (tester) async {
    var retried = false;
    await tester.pumpWidget(card(onRetry: () => retried = true));
    expect(find.text('The turn stopped'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.textContaining('Hermes lost the gateway'), findsOneWidget);
    await tester.tap(find.text('Retry'));
    expect(retried, isTrue);
  });

  testWidgets('Details shows the full message in mono, and hides it again', (tester) async {
    await tester.pumpWidget(card());
    expect(find.byKey(const ValueKey('failure-details')), findsNothing);
    await tester.tap(find.text('Details'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.byKey(const ValueKey('failure-details')), findsOneWidget);
    await tester.tap(find.text('Details'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.byKey(const ValueKey('failure-details')), findsNothing);
  });

  testWidgets('retrying shows the attempt and a Stop', (tester) async {
    var stopped = false;
    await tester.pumpWidget(card(retrying: true, attempt: 2, onStop: () => stopped = true));
    expect(find.text('Retrying…'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('attempt 2'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('Retry'), findsNothing);
    await tester.tap(find.text('Stop'));
    expect(stopped, isTrue);
  });
}
```

    backend.emit(const TurnFailed('run-1', 'boom'));

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement.** `ChatState.attempt` (default 1; in `copyWith`, in `withNewestPage`/`withEarlierPage` carry it; `withUserTurn` resets it to 1). `retry()` sets `attempt: value.attempt + 1` in its first `copyWith`. `TranscriptEntry` takes `attempt` and `onStop`, passes them to `_FailedCard`, which becomes stateful for the Details toggle:
  - normal: `IrisOrb(AgentState.failed, 28)` beside the title and cause (cause = the first line of `notice.text`, cut at 140 characters with an ellipsis); under them, when Details is open, `Container(key: ValueKey('failure-details'), …)` holding the whole text in `codeSmall` 11 on `surfaceSunken`; then the button row with `Retry` (`flex: 2`, failed fill, `palette.inkInverse` label, 44 high) and `Details` (`flex: 1`, ghost).
  - retrying: neutral card, `IrisOrb(AgentState.loading, 22)`, `Text('Retrying…')`, mono `attempt $attempt`, and a ghost `Stop` calling `onStop` (shown only when `onStop != null`).
  - In `chat_screen.dart` pass `attempt: state.attempt` and `onStop: () => unawaited(_chat.stop())` to the entry.

#### 12b — Rename, archive and the Archived screen (frame B3)

**Files:** `lib/features/chat/conversation_actions.dart` (`_RenameSheet`), `lib/design/components/toast.dart`, `lib/features/chat/conversation_list_screen.dart` (archive toast), `lib/features/chat/archived_screen.dart`; tests `test/features/archived_screen_test.dart`, `test/features/rename_sheet_test.dart`.

Deltas:
1. **Rename sheet:** title "Rename chat"; field 50 high, `surfaceSunken`, 1.5pt accent focus, text pre-selected (already); a clear button (44 hit, `Icons.close`) when non-empty; **Save** disabled (`onPressed: null`) when the text is empty or unchanged; Cancel.
2. **Toast duration:** `showIrisToast({…, Duration? visibleFor})`, default `IrisToast.visibleFor` (4 s); the archive toast passes `const Duration(seconds: 5)` ("longer than other toasts").
3. **Archived screen:** title `serif` 38 (was 34); a hint line under it, `inkMuted` mono: "Tap the box to bring a chat back to home. Long-press for Delete."; a count label `IrisLabel('${rows.length} chats')` above the rows; each row min 60 high on `surface` with a `line` hairline and radius 18; the unarchive control is an icon button (`Icons.unarchive_outlined`, 20, accent, 44×44) whose semantic label stays `Unarchive <title>`; empty state: `IrisOrb(AgentState.rest, size: 80, glow: false)`, serif 28 "Nothing archived.", body muted "Archived chats wait here until you bring them back."

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:iris/features/chat/conversation_actions.dart';

import '../support/harness.dart';

void main() {
  Future<void> open(WidgetTester tester, void Function(String?) done) async {
    await tester.pumpWidget(
      themed(
        Builder(
          builder: (context) => TextButton(
            onPressed: () async => done(await showRenameSheet(context, 'Weekly planning')),
            child: const Text('go'),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
    await tester.tap(find.text('go'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
  }

  testWidgets('Save does nothing until the title changes and is not empty', (tester) async {
    String? result = 'untouched';
    await open(tester, (value) => result = value);
    await tester.tap(find.text('Save'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('Save'), findsOneWidget, reason: 'unchanged: the sheet stays open');
    await tester.enterText(find.byType(EditableText), '');
    await tester.tap(find.text('Save'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('Save'), findsOneWidget, reason: 'empty: the sheet stays open');
    await tester.enterText(find.byType(EditableText), 'Q4');
    await tester.tap(find.text('Save'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(result, 'Q4');
  });

  testWidgets('the clear button empties the field', (tester) async {
    final semantics = tester.ensureSemantics();
    await open(tester, (_) {});
    await tester.tap(find.bySemanticsLabel('Clear'));
    await tester.pump();
    expect(tester.widget<EditableText>(find.byType(EditableText)).controller.text, isEmpty);
    semantics.dispose();
  });
}

#### 12c — Context ring, the 95% strip, and the usage thresholds (frames B6, B7)

**Files:** `lib/design/components/controls.dart` (`IrisRing`), `lib/features/chat/composer.dart` (`_ContextRing`), `lib/features/chat/chat_screen.dart` (strip), `lib/features/home/usage_strip.dart`, `lib/features/settings/usage_settings_screen.dart`; tests `test/features/context_ring_test.dart`, `test/features/usage_strip_test.dart`.

Design: ring 26 visual in a 44 hit, radius 11, stroke 3, round cap, track `lineStrong`, arc accent from 12 o'clock, easing on a 300/30 spring when a turn ends. Label hidden under 50%; mono 11.5 `inkMuted` from 50%; **at 90% and over the arc and the label switch to the needs-you colour**. The 95% strip sits above the composer, 44 high, needs-you 10% fill and 32% line, 13 ink: "Context is nearly full. Older turns will be summarised." with an accent **Branch** that starts a fresh chat with a summary. Meters are accent and switch to needs-you only when you have to act: usage bars at **85%** and over.

- [ ] **Step 1: Failing tests**

`test/features/context_ring_test.dart`:

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:iris/data/chat/chat_models.dart';
import 'package:iris/features/chat/composer.dart';

import '../support/harness.dart';

void main() {
  Future<void> pump(WidgetTester tester, double percent, {VoidCallback? onContext}) async {
    final controller = TextEditingController();
    addTearDown(controller.dispose);
    await tester.pumpWidget(themed(Align(
      alignment: Alignment.bottomCenter,
      child: Composer(
        controller: controller,
        running: false,
        onSend: () {},
        onStop: () {},
        contextUsage: ContextUsage(percent: percent),
        onContext: onContext,
      ),
    )));
  }

  testWidgets('the percentage stays hidden under 50%', (tester) async {
    await pump(tester, 49);
    expect(find.text('49%'), findsNothing);
    expect(find.bySemanticsLabel('Context window, 49 percent'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('from 50% the label shows in mono', (tester) async {
    await pump(tester, 62);
    expect(find.text('62%'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('the ring has a 44 point hit area', (tester) async {
    await pump(tester, 62, onContext: () {});
    final box = tester.getSize(find.bySemanticsLabel('Context window, 62 percent').first);
    expect(box.width, greaterThanOrEqualTo(44));
    expect(box.height, greaterThanOrEqualTo(44));
  });

  testWidgets('at 95% the strip offers Branch', (tester) async {
    var branched = false;
    await tester.pumpWidget(themed(ContextStrip(onBranch: () => branched = true)));
    expect(find.text('Context is nearly full. Older turns will be summarised.'), findsOneWidget);
    await tester.tap(find.text('Branch'));
    expect(branched, isTrue);
  });
}
```

#### 12d — Attachments: the "+" menu and chips (frame B8)

**Files:** `lib/features/chat/attachment_picker.dart`, `lib/features/chat/attachment_chip.dart`, `lib/features/chat/composer.dart`, `lib/features/chat/chat_screen.dart`, `lib/data/chat/attachment.dart` (thumbnail helper); tests `test/features/attachment_ui_test.dart`; update existing picker fakes.

Spec decision: **Photos and Files only, 20 MB** (the design's Camera row and 25 MB are not built).

Design: a menu anchored above "+" (the "+" rotates 45° to close): radius 18, raised, rows 48 high, a mono footnote "up to 5 files · 20 MB each". Chips 52 high, radius 14, surface + line, thumb 40 radius 10 (a photo fills it, cover; a file shows a doc glyph), name 12.5/500 (max 96), size mono 10.5, remove 40×44. The row scrolls sideways. At 5 files "+" is 38% opacity and disabled and mono "5 / 5 FILES" shows. The picker is asked for at most the remaining slots. A too-large file: a **top** toast, failed glyph, names the file and the limit, and the file is not added.

```dart
Future<List<Attachment>> pickFiles(AttachSource source) async {

The "+" is an `IrisPressable` that rotates 45° (`AnimatedRotation`, instant under reduced motion) while its menu is open; the menu is an `OverlayEntry`/dialog anchored above it (use the same `showGeneralDialog` approach as `showMessageMenu`, 2 rows: Photos `Icons.photo_outlined`, Files `Icons.folder_outlined`, a mono footnote "up to 5 files · 20 MB each"). #### 12e — Dictation (frame B9)

**Files:** `lib/features/chat/voice_recorder.dart`, `lib/features/chat/composer.dart` (`_RecordingStrip`, `_MicButton`), `lib/features/chat/chat_screen.dart`, test fakes; tests `test/features/dictation_ui_test.dart`.

Design: the mic is 44, `accentQuiet` with an accent glyph, shown only while the field is empty. Recording: the composer morphs into a **72-high bar**, Cancel (44) on the left, a **listening orb 56 whose wobble is the live level**, elapsed mono 12 in the listening hue, Stop (44, accent fill, square glyph). Transcribing: a thinking orb, the Stop slot kept empty so nothing shifts; the text drops into the field when done. Missed: a 48-high note above the composer, rest orb (no glow), "Didn't catch that." with an accent **Try again** that re-arms the mic, for 6 s. Reduced motion: the orb holds still and the level shows as ring weight in three steps (quiet / speaking / loud), updated at most four times a second.

First give the file's `FakeRecorder` (at the bottom of `test/features/chat_screen_test.dart`) a counter and a level stream:

```dart
final class FakeRecorder implements VoiceRecorder {
  bool allowed = true;
  bool recording = false;
  int starts = 0;
  final _level = StreamController<double>.broadcast();

  void speak(double level) => _level.add(level);

  @override
  Stream<double> get level => _level.stream;

  @override
  Future<bool> start() async {
    starts++;
    return recording = allowed;
  }

  @override
  Future<Uint8List?> stop() async {
    recording = false;
    return Uint8List.fromList([1]);
  }

  @override
  Future<void> cancel() async => recording = false;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement.**
  - `VoiceRecorder` gets `Stream<double> get level`. `RecordVoiceRecorder`: `_recorder.onAmplitudeChanged(const Duration(milliseconds: 100)).map((a) => ((a.current + 45) / 45).clamp(0.0, 1.0))` (dBFS −45…0 → 0…1), listened to only while recording. The test fake (`test/support/`, find it by `implements VoiceRecorder`) exposes a `StreamController<double>` as `level` and counts `starts`.
  - `Composer`: new `level` (`Stream<double>?`). While `recording != null` the whole composer box is replaced by `Container(key: ValueKey('recording-bar'), height: 72, …)` containing Cancel (44 hit, text), the listening orb, mono elapsed 12 in `palette.colorFor(AgentState.loading)`, Stop (44, accent fill, `SendGlyph(SendKind.stop)`, semantic label `Stop recording`). The orb is `IrisOrb(state: AgentState.loading, size: 56)` inside `AnimatedScale(key: ValueKey('level-orb'), scale: 1 + .15 * levelValue, duration: reduced ? zero : 100 ms)`; the level value is held in a `ValueNotifier<double>` fed by the stream and **throttled to 4 updates a second** under reduced motion (a `Timer`-free check: ignore an update arriving within 250 ms of the last one). Under reduced motion the orb does not scale; instead a ring of `Border.all(width: 1/2/3.5)` for quiet (<.2) / speaking (<.6) / loud shows the level in three steps.
  - Transcribing: `IrisOrb(AgentState.thinking, size: 56)` in the same bar, an empty Stop slot (a `SizedBox(44)`), label "Transcribing" (semantics).
  - `_MicButton`: 44×44 (`ensureTapTarget` default), `accentQuiet` fill, accent glyph.
  - `chat_screen`: replace the "Didn't catch that" toast with a note above the composer: `_missed` bool set when the transcript is empty; a 48-high row with `IrisOrb(AgentState.rest, size: 24, glow: false)`, the text, and **Try again** (accent) which clears `_missed` and calls `_startDictation()`; auto-clear after 6 s with a `Timer` cancelled in `dispose`.

#### 12f — Rich replies (frame B10)

**Files:** `lib/design/components/markdown.dart`, `lib/design/components/markdown_image.dart`, `lib/design/components/math.dart` (display fade), `lib/design/tokens/` (`IrisCodeColors` if it lives there; find it with grep); tests extend `test/design/markdown_test.dart`, `test/design/markdown_syntax_test.dart`.

Design deltas (compare each element with the frame and the existing Phase 5 rendering; change only what differs):
1. **Maths:** display block `surfaceSunken` with a `line` hairline, radius 14, padding 18/16, scrolls sideways with a **36 pt fade on the trailing edge** (a `ShaderMask` with a horizontal gradient to transparent over the last 36 pt, only when the content is wider than the box); inline maths at 1.1× body.
2. **Alerts:** radius 14, padding 10/12, the hue at 10% fill and 30% line; label mono 11 caps in the hue with a dot; body ink. Colours: Note `loading`, Tip `done`, Important `thinking`, Warning `working`, Caution `failed`. (The spec text earlier said Note `idle`; the design's Note is the connecting hue; follow the design.)
3. **Footnotes:** refs mono 11 accent superscript with a 44-point hit area from padding; the block under a hairline headed `NOTES` (mono caps), items 13/1.5 `inkMuted`.
4. **Images:** 4:3 card, radius 14; idle: `surfaceSunken`, dashed `lineStrong` border (`DashedRRectPainter`, Task 8), the host in Geist 12/500, the action "Tap to load" in mono accent; loading: connecting orb with a size note; loaded: the image with a mono name tag; failed: failed tint with "Couldn't load" and **Tap to retry**.
5. **Code block:** header 44 high: mono language in ink, then "detected" in `inkMuted` when the language was detected, then **Copy** 44 high in accent; body mono 12.5/1.7. **Syntax colours:** keywords in accent, comments in `inkMuted`, **every other token in ink** (no rainbow: colour is state). In `lib/design/tokens/code_colors.dart` derive `keyword` from `p.accent` (made legible on the well, as now) and keep `comment` as it is; set `string`, `number`, `title`, `type`, `attr` and `meta` to `plain`. `addition` and `deletion` keep their colours, because a diff's colour carries meaning. Update `test/design/markdown_syntax_test.dart` for the new mapping.

then:

```dart
  const threeChats = [
    ConversationSummary(id: 'a', title: 'Nightly backup to cold storage'),
    ConversationSummary(id: 'b', title: 'Investigate worker heartbeat gaps'),
    ConversationSummary(id: 'c', title: 'Hermes server runbook'),
  ];

  List<SearchHit> threeHits() => [
    SearchHit(
      storedId: 'a',
      title: 'Nightly backup to cold storage',
      matches: 4,
      lastActive: DateTime.now(),
      snippet: const [SnippetRun('…the '), SnippetRun('backup', matched: true), SnippetRun(' at 02:00 saturates the disk…')],
    ),
    SearchHit(
      storedId: 'b',
      title: 'Investigate worker heartbeat gaps',
      matches: 2,
      lastActive: DateTime.now(),
      snippet: const [SnippetRun('move the '), SnippetRun('backup', matched: true), SnippetRun(' to 04:30')],
    ),
    SearchHit(
      storedId: 'c',
      title: 'Hermes server runbook',
      matches: 1,
      lastActive: DateTime.now(),
      snippet: const [SnippetRun('Restore from the cold '), SnippetRun('backup', matched: true)],
    ),
  ];

  /// Types [query] and lets the 200 ms debounce and the server answer land.
  Future<void> type(WidgetTester tester, String query) async {
    await tester.enterText(find.byType(EditableText), query);
    await tester.pump(const Duration(milliseconds: 300));
    await tester.pump();
    await tester.pump(const Duration(milliseconds: 100));
  }

  bool marked(InlineSpan span, Color color) {
    var found = false;
    span.visitChildren((s) {
      if (s is TextSpan && s.style?.backgroundColor == color) found = true;
      return true;
    });
    return found;
  }

  testWidgets('results are headed by chats and matches, with each hit’s own count', (tester) async {
    backend.searchResult = Ok(threeHits());
    await tester.pumpWidget(home(threeChats));
    await settle(tester);
    await type(tester, 'backup');
    expect(find.text('3 CHATS · 7 MATCHES'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.textContaining('4 matches'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.textContaining('1 match'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('matched words are highlighted in the snippet', (tester) async {
    backend.searchResult = Ok(threeHits());
    await tester.pumpWidget(home(threeChats));
    await settle(tester);
    await type(tester, 'backup');
    final quiet = IrisThemeData(skin: IrisSkin.aperture, isDark: true).palette.accentQuiet;
    expect(
      find.byWidgetPredicate((w) => w is Text && w.textSpan != null && marked(w.textSpan!, quiet)),
      findsWidgets,
    );
  });

  testWidgets('an empty focused field shows recent searches, newest first', (tester) async {
    await tester.pumpWidget(
      home(threeChats, overrides: [recentSearchesProvider.overrideWith(() => _RecentAlready(['heartbeat', 'passkey']))]),
    );
    await settle(tester);
    await tester.tap(find.byType(EditableText));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('RECENT SEARCHES'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(
      tester.getTopLeft(find.text('heartbeat')).dx,
      lessThan(tester.getTopLeft(find.text('passkey')).dx),
    );
    await tester.tap(find.text('passkey'));
    await tester.pump();
    expect(tester.widget<EditableText>(find.byType(EditableText)).controller.text, 'passkey');
  });

  testWidgets('Clear forgets the recent searches', (tester) async {
    await tester.pumpWidget(
      home(threeChats, overrides: [recentSearchesProvider.overrideWith(() => _RecentAlready(['heartbeat']))]),
    );
    await settle(tester);
    await tester.tap(find.byType(EditableText));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    await tester.tap(find.text('Clear'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('heartbeat'), findsNothing);
  });

  testWidgets('a search that finds nothing says what was searched and how to retry', (tester) async {
    backend.searchResult = const Ok([]);
    await tester.pumpWidget(home(threeChats));
    await settle(tester);
    await type(tester, 'kubernetes');
    expect(find.text('No chats mention ‘kubernetes’.'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('Try fewer words or a different spelling.'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('a search that found something is remembered once', (tester) async {
    backend.searchResult = Ok(threeHits());
    await tester.pumpWidget(home(threeChats));
    await settle(tester);
    await type(tester, 'backup');
    final container = ProviderScope.containerOf(tester.element(find.byType(ConversationListScreen)));
    expect(container.read(recentSearchesProvider), ['backup']);
    await type(tester, '');
    await type(tester, 'backup');
    expect(container.read(recentSearchesProvider), ['backup']);
  });

  testWidgets('a search that found nothing is not remembered', (tester) async {
    backend.searchResult = const Ok([]);
    await tester.pumpWidget(home(threeChats));
    await settle(tester);
    await type(tester, 'kubernetes');
    final container = ProviderScope.containerOf(tester.element(find.byType(ConversationListScreen)));
    expect(container.read(recentSearchesProvider), isEmpty);
  });
```
In the implementation (Step 3), record the query in the debounce callback: after `setState(() => _serverQuery = query)`, `final hits = await ref.read(conversationSearchProvider(query).future)` inside a `try`, and `if (mounted && hits.isNotEmpty) ref.read(recentSearchesProvider.notifier).add(query)`; swallow a failure.

```dart
/// Which image hosts Iris loads without a tap, and how to add one. Provided
/// once at the app root from the person's saved list; absent in tests that
/// do not care, which means "none".
library;

import 'package:flutter/widgets.dart';

class ImageHostScope extends InheritedWidget {
  const ImageHostScope({
    required this.hosts,
    required this.onAllow,
    required super.child,
    super.key,
  });

  final Set<String> hosts;
  final ValueChanged<String> onAllow;

  static ImageHostScope? maybeOf(BuildContext context) =>
      context.dependOnInheritedWidgetOfExactType<ImageHostScope>();

  @override
  bool updateShouldNotify(ImageHostScope old) => old.hosts != hosts;
}
```

```dart
        case VaultStep(:final kind, :final name, :final site, :final hint):

          final answer = _secret = Completer<void>();
          _secretId = id;

            ),
          });

          _secret = null;
          _secretId = null;
          if (_stopped) return false;
        case ConnectStep(:final targets):
          final id = '$sessionId-connect-${_serial++}';
          final answer = _secret = Completer<void>();
          _secretId = id;
          final request = ConnectionRequest.parse(
            Json.of({'op_id': id, 'timeout_seconds': 300, 'targets': targets}),
            sessionId: sessionId,
          );
          if (request != null) emit(ConnectionAsked(request));
          await answer.future;
          _secret = null;
          _secretId = null;
          if (_stopped) return false;

### Task 16: Live write test

**Files:** Create `test/live/phase6_write_live_test.dart`.

**Interfaces:** consumes `DashboardChat` (all Task 1 methods) and the Task 0 probe result (skip a step the probe found missing, with a printed line, instead of failing).

It creates a throwaway chat on the real server, so it runs only when asked for:
`flutter test --tags live_write --run-skipped test/live/phase6_write_live_test.dart`. Replies are slow (about 95–160 s for a trivial prompt): each turn waits **at least 240 s**. Print shapes only, never content.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the test.** Use the setup of `phase5_live_test.dart` (same `~/.iris-live.json`). One sequential `test` (the steps depend on each other) with a 20-minute timeout:

```dart
@Tags(['live_write'])
library;

// imports as in phase5_live_test.dart, plus the Phase 6 types

void main() {
  late DashboardSession session;
  late DashboardChat chat;

  setUp(() async { /* identical to phase5_live_test.dart */ });

  Future<void> turnDone(String runtimeId, {Duration wait = const Duration(seconds: 300)}) async {
    await chat.events
        .where((e) => e.sessionId == runtimeId && (e is MessageCompleted || e is TurnFailed))
        .first
        .timeout(wait);
  }

  void log(String line) => print(line); // ignore: avoid_print

  test('pin, undo, branch, queue, delete on a throwaway chat', () async {
    // 1. create + a first turn
    final created = (await chat.create()).valueOrNull!;
    unawaited(chat.submit(created.runtimeId, 'Reply with exactly the word: pong'));
    await turnDone(created.runtimeId);
    log('first turn -> done');

    // 2. busy mode round trip (restores the original value)
    final original = (await chat.busyMode()).valueOrNull;
    log('busy mode -> ${original?.wire ?? 'unsupported'}');
    if (original != null) {
      expect((await chat.setBusyMode(BusyMode.queue)).isOk, isTrue);
      expect((await chat.busyMode()).valueOrNull, BusyMode.queue);
      await chat.setBusyMode(original);
    }

    // 3. queued submit while a turn runs: held by Hermes
    unawaited(chat.submit(created.runtimeId, 'Reply with exactly the word: ping'));
    await Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(seconds: 3));
    final queued = await chat.submit(created.runtimeId, 'Reply with exactly the word: again', queued: true);
    log('queued submit -> ${queued.valueOrNull?.name ?? 'failed: ${queued.failureOrNull?.message}'}');
    await turnDone(created.runtimeId);
    await turnDone(created.runtimeId);

    // 4. undo removes the last exchange
    final undone = await chat.undo(created.runtimeId);
    log('undo -> ${undone.isOk ? 'removed ${undone.valueOrNull}' : 'failed: ${undone.failureOrNull?.message}'}');
    if (undone.isOk) expect(undone.valueOrNull, greaterThan(0));

    // 5. branch the whole chat, then delete the branch
    final branched = await chat.branchWhole(created.storedId);
    log('branchWhole -> ${branched.isOk ? 'ok' : 'failed: ${branched.failureOrNull?.message}'}');
    if (branched.isOk) {
      final child = branched.valueOrNull!;
      expect(child.storedId, isNot(created.storedId));
      final live = await chat.activeSessions();
      log('active sessions -> ${live.valueOrNull?.length ?? 'unsupported'}');
    }

    // 6. delete is refused while live, then works after the session is closed
    final refused = await chat.delete(created.storedId);
    log('delete while live -> ${refused.isOk ? 'accepted' : 'refused: ${refused.failureOrNull?.statusCode}'}');
  }, timeout: const Timeout(Duration(minutes: 20)));
}
```
Adjust the sequence to what Task 0's probe recorded (skip, with a log line, anything the server lacks). The test must clean up: after the assertions, try `chat.delete` for every conversation it created (the branch child too) and print whether the cleanup succeeded; a leftover throwaway chat is reported to the user rather than hidden.
- [ ] **Step 2: Run it** (it takes several minutes) and record the printed lines in `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-16-phase6-device.md` under "Live write test".
